- **Lot 239 (contenu et règles ★, M10d du plan des mods : vanilla créé, sans changement pour pd2)** : mercenaires de Diablo II LoD 1.13c dans la base (l'entrée « M10d non fait » ci-dessous date du lot M10 ; elle est levée). Données : `src/data/mercs-d2.ts`, généré par `tools/vanilla/gen.ts` (table `mercs`, contrôlée par `npm run vanilla`) depuis Hireling.txt (extension) : une ligne par sous-type, difficulté d'embauche et palier de niveau (54 lignes ; les deux sous-types identiques des Barbares n'en font qu'un). Types de la base (`MERCS`, registre `mercs`) : Éclaireuse (Feu, Froid), Garde du désert (Prière / Défi / Visée bénie en Normal et en Enfer, Épines / Gel sacré / Puissance en Cauchemar : champ `hire` des sous-types), Loup de fer (Feu, Froid, Foudre), Barbare ; armes de D2, aucun objet de classe ; `unlockLevel` devient une donnée (`unlock`, `mercUnlockLevel`). Règle nommée `merc.model` (src/mods/d2/mercs.ts, d'après MonsterAI.cpp et AiThink.cpp de D2MOO) : ligne d'embauche (sous-type, difficulté, palier), caractéristiques + gains par niveau (force, dextérité, dégâts en huitièmes, résistances en quarts), compétences au niveau requis, niveau Level + LvlPerLvl / 32, poids de tirage DefaultChance et Chance + ChancePerLvl / 4 (auras et Armure gelée permanentes), sous-types proposés par difficulté, casque / armure / arme (bouclier du Loup de fer, épées à une main), difficulté d'embauche enregistrée. Sauvegarde : champ `merc.hire` (difficulté d'embauche, écrit seulement par un modèle qui l'enregistre : D2) ; migration des anciennes parties de base (sous-types PD2) au premier accès : Ascendant → Loup de fer, Éclaireuse physique → feu, Barbares → Coup violent. Compétences de mercenaire ajoutées à la simulation : Vision intérieure, Inferno, Pointe glaciale, Éclair chargé, Étourdissement, Armure gelée (défense permanente) ; sans tables de mercenaire, les coups lisent les chiffres de la compétence de classe (`mtab(…, key)`). PD2 : `src/data/mercs-pd2.gen.ts` → `mods/pd2/data/mercs.gen.ts`, modèle PD2 (tableaux et paliers du wiki, probabilités fixes, tous les emplacements, liste d'équipement du wiki, anciens sous-types) dans `mods/pd2/rules/mercs.ts`, types et sous-types PD2 en patch généré par diff (`mods/pd2/data/mercs.patch.ts` : 4 patchés, Ascendant ajouté, identifiant historique). Limites : le niveau du mercenaire reste celui du personnage (modèle du moteur : pas d'expérience propre), les cadences et formes d'attaque sont celles du moteur, le coût d'embauche reste celui du moteur. Bots : seuil de morts du Druide sans le mod relevé à 10 (la Tempête de feu de D2 du lot M9g ne protège pas le bot ours-garou avant le niveau 6 ; plans et seuils par jeu de règles : lot M13). Tests : `tests/unit/vanilla-hirelings.test.ts` ; `merc.test.ts` (sous-types PD2) passe dans `mods/pd2/tests/unit/`. Contrôles allégés : tsc, ESLint, Prettier, suite `unit` complète avec et sans le mod, bots sans le mod, `vanilla-diff` (mercs 5 : identique), `npm run vanilla`, empreintes `registries`, `mercs`, `saves`, `chars`, `bots` (référence 3d8e5d0) : comportement identique.

- **Lot 241 (architecture et contenu ★, M13a du plan des mods : bots par jeu de règles, sans changement pour pd2)** : règle nommée `bots.profile` (src/mods/rules.ts, interface `BotRules` : src/mods/d2/bots.ts, exportée par l'API des mods) : plans de compétences des builds, listes de compétences que le cerveau traite à part (zones, effets posés et leur nombre de coups, effets sans cumul, dégâts sur la durée relancés, armures entretenues, invocations sur cadavre), part de vie sous laquelle le Champ statique ne sert plus, mercenaire voulu (`rehire` : reprendre aussi pour un autre sous-type), préparation du Cube et des cartes d'endgame (`townCube`, `prepareMap`, avec une vue `BotTown` du cerveau), charmes de l'arène, seuils des tests de bots (`checks`). Cerveau (`src/sim/bot/brain.ts`) : `BUILD_PLANS` remplacé par `buildPlan(build)` et `botRules()`, plus aucun identifiant propre à PD2 (19 identifiants et 2 préfixes de moins dans le résiduel : brain.ts et arena.ts n'y figurent plus) ; `runePlan` inchangé. Base (Diablo II LoD 1.13c) : 18 plans classiques écrits pour les arbres de D2 (prérequis de Skills.txt : Loup-garou avant l'Ours-garou, Flèche de froid avant la Flèche guidée, Maîtrise des griffes avant la Rafale de vitesse, Endurance accrue avant la Vitesse accrue, malédictions intermédiaires du Nécromancien… ; les plans PD2 nommaient des compétences absentes de D2 ou sans leurs prérequis de D2, si bien que le bot ours-garou de la base n'apprenait jamais l'Ours-garou et mettait ses points en Lycanthropie), listes de D2 (Armure gelée, tirs des tourelles de D2 : 10 / 5 / 10, plancher StaticFieldMin de DifficultyLevels.txt + 5 %), mercenaire de D2 (Garde du désert : Puissance en mêlée, Gel sacré pour les lanceurs en Cauchemar, reprise pour ces deux sous-types ; sinon Visée bénie / Défi), seuils du lot 239 (niveau 6, 10 morts de Druide). Mod pd2 (`mods/pd2/bots/` : `plans.ts`, `maps.ts`, `index.ts`) : plans S13, cartes d'endgame du bot (`prepareMap`, `zoneImmuneShare`, `GOOD_ZONE_SHARE` : déplacés tels quels), Combustion, Hydre mineure, Enchantement du froid, Tourelle d'éclairs en chaîne, archers squelettes et Profanation dans les listes, tirs PD2 des tourelles, plancher PD2 du Champ statique, mercenaire de l'usage PD2, boîtes à énigme, charmes de brisure de l'arène, seuils 12 / 3. SDK : `computeDerived`. Tests : `tests/unit/bot-plans.test.ts` (chaque plan nomme des compétences de l'arbre de sa classe ; base : au niveau 35, chaque compétence des 30 premiers points du plan ouverte au niveau 24 a reçu un point), `mods/pd2/tests/unit/bots-pd2.test.ts` (profil du mod, maîtrise à une main du Tourbillon, sortie de balance-builds), `tests/bots/progression.test.ts` lit ses seuils dans le profil. Contrôles allégés : tsc, ESLint, Prettier, suite `unit` avec et sans le mod, bot de fumée vanilla, résiduel, empreintes `chars`, `mercs`, `skills`, `bots`, `endgame` (référence 3d8e5d0) : identiques.
- **Lot 242 (règles et bots ★, M13b du plan des mods : seuils des bots par jeu de règles, causes des morts vanilla, sans changement pour pd2)** : enquête sur le seuil assoupli au lot 239 (10 morts de Druide sans le mod, 8 morts de l'ours-garou avant le niveau 6). Mesuré, bots de progression sans le mod (graine 1, 45 min) : deux causes, aucune dans les chiffres de Skills.txt (la Tempête de feu de D2 fait bien 3 × 1,2 à 2,3 dégâts de feu par langue au niveau 1 : EMin 3, HitShift 2, par seconde de flamme ; elle est faible en D2 aussi). (1) Stratégie du bot : le plan PD2 de l'ours-garou n'avait pas le Loup-garou, prérequis de l'Ours-garou en D2 : le bot n'apprenait jamais la forme et mettait ses points en Lycanthropie (plans de D2 du lot 241). (2) Défaut du moteur commun aux deux jeux : tout coup reçu mettait le joueur en récupération après coup ; D2 ne le fait qu'à partir de 1/12 de la vie maximale (un Déchu qui frappe pour 2 bloquait un Barbare de 60 points de vie). Nouvelle règle nommée `combat.playerHitRecovery` : base = seuil de D2 (`src/mods/d2/combat.ts`), mod pd2 = tout coup (modèle d'avant, gardé). Avec les deux : 1 à 2 morts de Druide avant le niveau 12 en 45 min (ours-garou 2, contre 8), seuil des morts de Druide sans le mod ramené à 3 (celui du jeu pd2). Autres défauts trouvés et corrigés par la mesure : la Valkyrie de la base appliquait la Frappe puissante de PD2, qui lit des chiffres que la compétence de D2 n'a pas, et arrêtait la simulation (bot Amazone à l'arc, nouvel indicateur `valkyrieStrikes` de la règle `skills.amazon` : coups critiques et Frappe puissante de la Valkyrie, PD2 seulement) ; le bot de la base relançait sans fin ses armures de glace l'une après l'autre (une seule active à la fois : `ARMOR_BUFFS`), vidant son mana : groupes d'armures exclusives dans `bots.profile` (`buffs`) ; le bot de la base vise d'abord les monstres qui ressuscitent leur groupe (chamans des Déchus : `focusRevivers`, attaques `revive` des monstres). Seuils de la base (`checks`, mesurés) : niveau 9 en 45 min (Sorcière 10 à 12, Druides 15 à 18), 3 morts de Druide, bot de combat réactivé sans le mod (8 groupes au plus restés debout en Normal et Cauchemar, 5 mesurés : groupes de trois champions de grande vie en Normal au niveau 20, la Sorcière de D2 vide son mana avant eux ; 84 avant la correction des armures), campagne (nouveau test de `tests/bots/vanilla-smoke.test.ts` : le Druide feu tue Baal en Normal en moins de 6 h de jeu ; mesuré 4 h 20, 4 h 47, 4 h 48 sur trois graines). Mesures de la base sans le mod sur 6 h de jeu (graine 1) : Druide feu niveau 50, Baal du Normal à 4 h 20 puis le Cauchemar (12 h : niveau 73, Baal du Cauchemar à 7 h 41, 1 tentative sur 5, l'Enfer dès 8 h 10) ; Druides vent et invocateur, Nécromancien des os niveau 34 (Normal, jusqu'au Chaos ; l'invocateur tue Baal à 8 h 11, le Nécromancien 0 sur 31 tentatives en 12 h) ; Assassin 30, Paladin 31, ours-garou 27 ; Sorcière 20 (201 morts en 219 runs), Barbare 19 (432 sur 434), Amazone à l'arc 15 (294 sur 297). Trône de Baal en Cauchemar avec le personnage de référence de l'arène (niveau 60, kit rare, 3 essais par build) : base 2 réussites sur 21 (Sorcière 1, Amazone 1), jeu pd2 11 sur 21. Écart avec D2 non comblé (documenté, docs/internal/MODS-RESIDUEL.md) : les classes de mêlée et d'arc et la Sorcière meurent presque à chaque run tant qu'elles farment Tristram (zone au niveau du personnage, groupes denses) et la Comtesse, seules runs ouvertes en début de partie (les zones de campagne ne sont pas proposées aux bots) ; économie de D2 sans multiplicateur (45 min : 4 000 pièces d'or ramassées contre 104 000 dans le jeu pd2), courbes de vie et de dégâts des monstres du moteur calibrées sur le jeu pd2. Tests : `vanilla-combat.test.ts` (seuil 1/12), `combat-systems.test.ts` (au seuil, et sous le seuil selon le jeu), `vanilla-skills-amazon.test.ts` (Valkyrie de D2 dans l'arène). Contrôles allégés : tsc, ESLint, Prettier, suite `unit` avec et sans le mod, bots de progression, de combat et de fumée sans le mod, résiduel (43 tests sautés sans le mod, contre 45), empreintes `chars`, `mercs`, `skills`, `bots`, `endgame` (référence 3d8e5d0) : identiques.

Chaîne type Vercel → Cloudflare → tunnel : par la réécriture, `CF-Connecting-IP` est l'adresse de sortie de Vercel (supposé d'après les documentations, non mesuré de bout en bout) ; réglages et options (appel direct de l'hôte du tunnel, non fait) dans `docs/EXPLOITATION-IP.md`. Verrouillage par un tiers : jeton aléatoire de l'appareil (`d2m.sync.device`, 32 octets, envoyé par `register`, `login` et chaque appel authentifié ; le compte garde l'empreinte SHA-256 `acct:<clé>:<jeton>`, 10 appareils au plus, champ `devices` facultatif compatible avec les anciens serveurs et clients) : un appareil reconnu ignore le verrou du compte, ses échecs sont comptés à part et il est oublié après 5 ; un appareil nouveau devient reconnu à la première réussite hors verrou. Stockage : plafond global de comptes (`SYNC_MAX_ACCOUNTS`, défaut 200 ; compteur `meta/accounts.json` en écriture conditionnelle, recréé en comptant `accounts/` s'il manque, place rendue à la suppression du compte ; existence lue avant de réserver : un `409` ne coûte pas d'écriture) ; 16 Mo de sauvegardes par compte (`SYNC_MAX_ACCOUNT_BYTES`, `413 quota` seulement si la taille augmente) ; `stage` : une copie par emplacement, taille ≤ sauvegarde + 1 Ko. Pire cas ≈ 3,2 Go (au lieu d'illimité). Non fait : expiration des comptes inutilisés, règle de pare-feu Vercel (documentées dans SECURITY.md). Tests : verrou par un tiers sur un appareil reconnu et inconnu, échecs propres à l'appareil, 10 appareils, plafond de comptes et recomptage, plafond par compte, `stage` borné, moteur réel avec un compte verrouillé.

- **Lot 256 (mobile, Q2, audit qualité du 19/10 §1.2, §1.3, §2.8 : pliables, sans changement de jeu)** : constats vérifiés au navigateur headless (nouveau `tools/e2e/fold.mjs`, tailles CSS du Fold plié 344×882 / 882×344, déplié 884×1104 / 1104×884, ancien Fold déplié 673×841 ; captures avant / après hors dépôt). (1) Écran « tournez l'appareil » : sur le Fold récent déplié (884 px de large) il ne s'affichait déjà pas (constat faux pour ce modèle) ; il bloquait l'ancien Fold déplié en portrait (673 px, rapport 0,8). Règle : portrait, `max-aspect-ratio: 3/4` et `max-width: 700px` (téléphone ou pliable fermé seulement) ; message déjà traduit par la couche i18n du DOM (constat faux), mais texte et conseil iPhone regroupés (ils étaient aux deux bouts de l'écran) et conseil « Sur l'écran d'accueil » limité à iOS (classe `ios` posée au démarrage). (2) Cibles tactiles : vérifié, en `pointer: coarse` la fermeture des panneaux D2 restait à 36 px (30 px sur écran bas) et les onglets à 34 px (sélecteurs plus spécifiques) ; zone de toucher élargie à 44 px par `::after` sans changer l'affichage (onglets : `overflow: clip` + `overflow-clip-margin` au lieu de `hidden`, qui coupait la zone ; écran bas : le panneau découpe avec 6 px de marge) ; mesuré par `elementFromPoint` : 44 px partout (avant : 34 / 30). (3) Capacités de pointage relues sur `matchMedia change` (`watchCapabilities`, test) : souris branchée ou débranchée, pliable plié / déplié. Non fait : borne haute de `--s` du HUD (sur le Fold déplié, 1,25 donne déjà des boutons physiquement plus grands que sur le téléphone plié, 0,88 ; l'augmenter mangerait la vue sans gain au toucher). `mobile.mjs` : appareils `fold-plie` et `fold-ouvert` (paysage). Changelog. **À confirmer sur l'appareil réel** (tailles CSS et rendu de `overflow-clip-margin`, absent de Safari : repli = comportement d'avant).
- **Lot 257 (performance, Q2bis, audit qualité du 19/10 §2 PF1, PF3, PF5 : rendu et HUD, sans changement de simulation)** : mesuré dans Chromium headless (rendu logiciel, processeur ralenti ×4, Sorcière 99 et bot en combat à Tristram, 20 s ; script de profil hors dépôt qui chronomètre les fonctions par image), avant → après : contrôle des planches `classSheetsChanged` + `missingSheetViews` 0,30 → 0,01 ms par image (constat PF1 vérifié : appelés à chaque image ; maintenant une fois par seconde au plus, toujours tout de suite après un changement de zone ou de planche), `Hud.updateLabels` 3,35 → 0,40-0,46 ms (PF3 vérifié : `offsetWidth` lu après les écritures de l'image ; largeur mesurée à la création et quand l'apparence ou le texte traduit change, `Map` des objets au lieu de `find`), `Hud.update` 2,27 → 1,68-1,90 ms (écritures de texte et de style seulement au changement : `src/ui/dom-memo.ts`, `setText` comparé au texte source car la couche i18n remplace le texte affiché ; nœuds `.qty` / `.cd` / `.t` gardés en mémoire ; test). PF5 : effets de malédiction qui suivent le monstre lui-même au lieu d'une recherche dans tous les monstres par image (non mesuré séparément). Non fait : PF2 (groupe de rendu du sol essayé : écart dans le bruit du rendu logiciel, 16,8 à 19,9 ms par image ; risque d'ordre d'affichage, à mesurer sur un vrai GPU), PF4, PF6, PF7 (allocations, pools, culling : gains non mesurables ici), PF8 (minicarte : 0,40 ms par image mesuré, faible). Trouvé par `mobile.mjs` sur le Fold plié en paysage (882×344) : la Téléportation chevauchait le bouton Réglages ; elle passe à sa gauche quand ils se touchent (validation mobile 64 / 64 sur fold-plie, iphone, fold-ouvert, android-petit). Changelog.
- **Lot 258 (fiabilité, Q3, audit qualité du 19/10 §2.5, §2.6, §3.9 : entrées / sorties, sans changement de jeu)** : constats vérifiés. (1) `saveShared` réécrivait `.backup` avant de comparer : chaque sauvegarde automatique sans changement faisait deux écritures inutiles et, pire, remplaçait la copie de secours par la version courante ; la comparaison passe avant (test qui compte les `setItem` : 0 sur 5 appels inchangés, copie = version d'avant le dernier changement ; le test « copie valide seulement » change d'un coffre identique à un coffre modifié pour garder son sens). (2) `render/assets.ts` : fichier témoin `verify` en 404 ou en HTML = pas de demande de mot de passe (avant : trois essais toujours « incorrects »). (3) `sync.ts` : délai de 30 s (`SYNC_TIMEOUT_MS`, lecture de la réponse comprise, abandon = « hors ligne » ; aucun délai pour l'envoi `keepalive` de fermeture ; tests en temps simulé). (4) `sw-template.js` : mise en cache des planches et modules sous `waitUntil`, échec (quota) attrapé et signalé comme pour les fichiers D2 (avant : rejet non géré) ; test, et attente `setTimeout(5)` du test de quota remplacée par `vi.waitFor` (Q4.4). (5) `guild.ts` : objet retiré mais ni placé ni remis au coffre (réseau coupé) gardé en note `redeposit` avant l'appel, redéposé à la reprise avec la même clé (test) ; erreur définitive journalisée. - **Lot 270 (U1, home screen: the Diablo logo no longer moves)**: owner report "the Diablo writing moves on the home screen". Reproduced headless (dev server with art generated by an earlier version, 40 captures 150 ms apart of the main menu, element box and `background-position` logged): the element never moves, the content of the animation frames does. Cause: `tools/d2import/export-ui.ts` built each frame of `logo-fire` by placing the two DC6 halves (`D2LogoFireLeft/Right`) by their frame size (left half at x 0, right half at the left width − 2, both aligned on the bottom of the cell) instead of their DC6 offsets; the flames change size from frame to frame, so the letters shifted by up to 8 px horizontally and 8 px vertically, 12 times per second (offsets measured on the MPQ: left `ox` −174 to −180, right `ox` −4 to +4, right bottom +54 to +62). The static letters layer (`D2LogoBlackLeft/Right`) was ignored, the fire was drawn opaque (not additive) and the band was placed by its top-left corner (the "II" covered "Expansion Set"). Fix: new `logo` sheet (`logoSheet`): letters then fire (additive blend baked as opacity = brightest channel) at their DC6 offsets around one anchor (`ax`, `ay` in ui.json), placed at (400, 120) on the 800 × 600 menu as in the game (credits: (400, 100) at 0.8); the select screen no longer shows the logo (the real screen has none). Already generated art: ui.json now has `rev` (`UI_REVISION` = 2, `tools/d2import/ui-keys.ts`); on-device generation treats an older `ui` as not ready and rebuilds only the interface (the "Interface" preparation step, a few seconds), never the rest; until then, and for art generated on a PC by the CLI before this lot, `d2Logo()` shows the first frame of the old sheet, still. Art generated before this lot: regenerate it with `npx tsx tools/d2import/cli.ts game icons` (interface and icons only). Tests: `export-ui-logo.test.ts` (letters at the same place in every frame, anchor, additive blend, revision). Captures (local, unversioned notes): before / after strips of four consecutive frames.
- **Lot 271 (U2, the mode is asked at launch)**: owner request: choose the mode first ("Diablo II (original)", "Project Diablo 2", any future ruleset or game mode), then only that mode's characters. `launchModes` (`charselect-core.ts`): each playable content ruleset (default overhaul first; the base is named "Diablo II (original)" by the app), then each game mode on each content it accepts (`d2+survivals` "Diablo II (original) — Survivals"); shown only with more than one (otherwise the screens behave as before: one list, every character). Diablo II screens: on the main menu one button per mode replaces "Single Player", under "Choose a game mode", the session's mode highlighted; Exit on the list goes back to it. Screen without the Diablo II art: a choice-of-mode screen first (description, character count per mode, import, sync, mod buttons), "Change mode" on the list and on the creation screen. Remembered: `d2m.mode` (`launchMode`, `platform.ts`), activated at boot (`bootRuleset`: pending creation, then the mode, then the last character's ruleset, then the default; an unknown mode is ignored); picking another mode stores it, sets the one-shot `sessionStorage` flag `d2m.openList` and reloads (one ruleset per page session, unchanged decision), the list then opens directly. Loading a character of another ruleset (automated browsers, `?class=`) also stores its ruleset as the mode, otherwise the remembered mode would be activated again at each reload. `charsForMode`: exact ruleset; a character that cannot be played (mod missing) is listed greyed in the mode of its content ruleset, or in the first mode when its content is missing, so it is never hidden, never rewritten (rule unchanged). Creation is always in the session's mode: the ruleset / game mode buttons of both creation screens are removed (`nextContent`, `nextMode`, `pickLabels`… deleted); `pendingCreate` stays for compatibility. Saves (v7), shared stash, guild per ruleset, storage migration unchanged. Docs: `MODS.md` (creation section, `menu.buttons`), `STORAGE.md` (`d2m.mode`, `d2m.openList`). Tests: `charselect.test.ts` rewritten (modes, per-mode lists, every character in exactly one mode, remembered mode and boot order, one-shot flag, choice screen, reload case, single-mode case, greyed characters, mod buttons).
- **Lot 272 (U3, character selection laid out as in Diablo II)**: owner report "the selection screen does not look like the real one". Before: a 4 × 2 grid of standing heroes with name plates under them, page buttons, Import / Sync on the screen, deletion by a 1.5 s hold, logo in a corner; on a folded Fold in portrait the 800 × 600 stage shrank to 344 × 258 above a tall dock (slots about 40 px). Now `charlist.ts` (one builder for both screens) reproduces LoD 1.13c: selected character's name in the top banner (and the mode name when there is a choice), 8 slots in 2 columns × 4 rows (standing character, name, "Level N Class", highest difficulty in green, or the reason in red when the mod is missing), the game's selection frame (`charselect-box`) on the selected slot, "Create New Character" and "Delete Character" under the frame, "Exit" and "OK" at the bottom, deletion confirmed by a "Are you sure…? No / Yes" box, double click or double tap (two taps within 450 ms on the same slot, `isDoubleTap`) plays, arrow keys / Enter / Escape, scrolling by rows of two past 8 characters (▲ ▼, wheel, vertical swipe; `select-core.ts`: `clampTop`, `revealTop`, `keepVisible`, `scrollInfo`, `moveSelection`). Two layouts of the same elements (`selectLayout`): `stage`, at the game's coordinates on the 800 × 600 screen (frame at 37, 86, slots 272 × 93, tall buttons at 33 / 433, 468, Exit 33, 537, OK 627, 537), for a mouse when the screen is shown at 90 % or more; `compact`, on the whole screen (touch screens, small windows): banner, framed 2 × 4 grid sized to the screen, 44 px scroll column, buttons 2 × 2 in portrait and on one row in landscape, every target ≥ 44 px (measured by `elementFromPoint` in `fold.mjs` on the four Fold sizes; on the folded Fold in landscape the slots are 400 × 55 px), text sized to the slot (container query units). The layout is redrawn when the window changes class. Characters: the class's standing animation from the player's files (`hero-<class>-nu1`, the creation screen's), animated on the selected slot, still on the others, scaled to the slot and slightly brightened (they are drawn for the campfire light); without the Diablo II art (public build), the class icon and frames drawn by the style sheet. Not reproduced: the character in its own equipment (the game's composite needs the core and every item's part sheets before the session starts; the creation-screen animation of the class is shown instead), the title by difficulty completed ("Slayer"…) and the "Expansion Character" line (every character is LoD here: the highest difficulty reached is shown instead), Convert / Battle.net (omitted as asked). Import and Sync stay on the main menu (Diablo II screens) or under the list (screen without the art, no choice of mode). Compatibility classes kept for the browser tests (`d2plate` / `char`, `ok` / `primary` / `danger`, class id); obsolete styles of the former list removed. Tests: `charlist.test.ts` (pure logic, view: order, framing, texts, buttons, scrolling, confirmation, keyboard; Diablo II screens with fake art: mode buttons, anchored logo, list of the mode, stage layout on a large window with a mouse, double tap, Exit; old logo shown still), `charselect.test.ts` adapted. E2E: `ruleset-choice.mjs` rewritten for the mode flow (with and without the art), `charselect.mjs` adapted, `fold.mjs` (12 characters, 2 × 4 grid, targets ≥ 44 px, captures of the menu and the selection on each size), `pc.mjs` (stage layout with a mouse, arrow keys, deletion asks and "No" keeps). The real Fold rendering is to be confirmed on the device. Classic-flow browser tests updated for the choice of mode (`classes`, `skilltree`, `d2-sprites`, `d2-shots`, `d2-spells`, `public-build`: the first mode when the choice is shown); new `d2-ui-revision.mjs` (on-device art: an interface of revision 1 is generated again alone after a reload, 464 → 464 cache entries, `logo` loaded). End-of-lot control (lots 270-272, on this commit): `npm run test:private` with the mod 2 290 passed (43 skipped), without 1 756 (45 skipped); fingerprint against the 3d8e5d0 reference: every behaviour section identical (registries: the same 479 differences and 142 moves to the mod), and no difference at all with the lot 268 file; coverage without private files 59.56 / 54.94 / 57.13 / 61.24 % (statements, branches, functions, lines; thresholds 57 / 53 / 53 / 58); `npm run vanilla` 25 files identical to the MPQ; `mods:suppression` OK; residual regenerated (`src/ui` 191 → 195 mentions: the changelog lines naming Project Diablo 2); typecheck, ESLint, Prettier; owner and public builds, `test:build`; e2e: `ruleset-choice.mjs` (with and without the art) OK, `charselect.mjs` (with and without the art) OK, `fold.mjs` 5 sizes OK, `mobile.mjs --quick` OK, `pc.mjs` OK, `classes.mjs` OK, `public-build.mjs` OK, `d2-onboard.mjs` 28 / 28, `d2-ui-revision.mjs` OK.
- **Lot 273 (S0, game-mode hooks in the base, no gameplay change)**: owner's decisions for the "Survivals" mode (design study of 19/10): Diablo II first (`d2+survivals`; `pd2+survivals` later, door left open), Diablo progression kept (levels, acts, the game's super-uniques and bosses, loot, difficulties), a skill of the D2 tree at each level, death keeps level, gear and progress and resumes at the last checkpoint, closed arena; recommendations kept: semi-automatic control by default (manual / semi / auto), 6 to 7 rounds an act of about 9 min, D2's death penalty, skill points only through the level-up screen during rounds, loot and experience rates of the content, arena cut from the real D2 map with a synthetic fallback, stats by automatic profile, about 90 live monsters at most; addendum: playable in portrait and in landscape. Hooks added, all inert without a mode (docs/MODS.md, "Game-mode hooks"): `RunDef.director` (per-run object made at the run's first step, `tick` every step from `RunState.update`, `onKill` before the run's end check), rules `game.autopilot` (before each `world.step`, after the controls), `skills.offer` (queue `Game.offers`, `takeOffer(id | null)`, `rerollOffer`, offers still waiting drawn again after a choice; `BotBrain.spendSkillPoints` answers them with its plan and spends kept points only where `skills.manualLearn` allows), `skills.manualLearn` (`Game.canLearnManually`, read by the skill tree), `death.xpPenalty` (D2 formula moved to `src/mods/d2/progression.ts`, `deathXpLoss`), `ui.orientation` (`'any'`: class `any-orientation` lifts the "rotate your device" prompt, fullscreen locks the screen to `any`: `setOrientationPolicy`), `render.zoom`; UI slot `run.complete`; level-up screens one at a time (`LevelUpGate`: levels gained while a mod screen is open wait for it). SDK: bots (`BotBrain`, `buildPlan`, `botRules`, `BUILD_CLASS`), `autoAim`, `SUPER_UNIQUE_DEFS`, `REGISTRIES`. Save format unchanged (v7; a mode's progress lives in `progression.milestones`). Fingerprints (worktree of the lot): pd2 against 3d8e5d0, every behaviour section identical (chars 1584, mercs 195, loot 2257, saves 3722, zones 2922, endgame 942, skills 1104, bots 270; registries: the known 479 differences and 142 moves); pd2 against the state before the lot: no difference on any of the 9 sections; d2 against the state before the lot: no difference (registries 17733, chars 1584, mercs 180, loot 3034, saves 3722, zones 2700, skills 1080, bots 270; `endgame` does not run in d2: maps belong to pd2). Controls: `test:private` (both passes), `npm run vanilla`, `mods:suppression`, owner and public builds, e2e mobile (112 ✓), fold (108 ✓), ruleset-choice (15 ✓), charselect (7 ✓), pc (32 ✓, 1 ✗ "left click on a monster attacks it": the same failure on 70dedf1, before the lot, not caused by it). Tests: `tests/unit/mods-gamemode.test.ts`.
- **Lot 274 (S1, Survivals mode skeleton)**: `mods/survivals/` (manifest `kind: 'gamemode'`, `rulesets: ['d2']`, content chunk): settings `Settings.mods.survivals` (control manual / semi / auto, default semi; live monster cap 60 / 90 / 120, default 90; stat profile balanced / vitality / manual; skill cards chosen or automatic), portrait allowed (`ui.orientation`), camera never narrower than 12 world units across the shorter side, not below 0.6 (`render.zoom`: 0.72 on a phone, 0.63 on a folded Fold in landscape, unchanged on the unfolded Fold), "how to play" button (`menu.buttons`), new characters in the Rogue Encampment (`create.character`). The mode is offered at launch as "Diablo II (original) — Survivals" (lot 271 machinery). Discovered like pd2 (`import.meta.glob`, `MODS`), never imported by the base (`d2m/mod-boundary`). Displayed texts: French source text and `mods/survivals/i18n/en.json`, as the whole game (the DOM translation layer translates French into English; English is the default language). Tests (`mods/survivals/tests/unit`, each in a fresh module graph with `d2+survivals` active): inert in `d2` alone (no rule replaced, every slot empty), refused on `pd2`, active in `d2+survivals`, content rules untouched, translations complete and never overriding the base's.
- **Lot 275 (S2, director and arenas of Act I)**: seven rounds (runs `survivals:a1r1` to `a1r7`, hidden, `campaign: 'act1'`: back to the Rogue Encampment): Blood Moor + Den of Evil (Corpsefire, the Den's skill point at the first clear), Cold Plains + Burial Grounds (Bishibosh halfway, Blood Raven), Stony Field (Rakanishu), Dark Wood + Black Marsh (Treehead Woodfist, the Countess), Tamoe Highland + Monastery + Barracks (the Smith), Jail + Cathedral (Pitspawn Fouldog, Boneash), Andariel's Lair (guards, then Andariel at 1:30, `rewardAs: 'andariel'`). Arena: the 64 × 64 window of the player's D2 map of the zone with the most open ground reachable from its middle (summed-area table, 4-connected flood), closed by four invisible walls (`blockers`), the rest of the map drawn but out of reach; less than 35 % open (dungeons) or no map: arena built with the zone's look (border, 6 to 10 obstacles away from the border and the middle); 40 × 40 for the boss. Director (`RunDef.director`): target population by segment (0 for 3 s, 12 → 30 to 1:00, 30 → 50, 45 with the encirclement, breather of 25 at 5:00, 50 → 80 from 5:30 with the other zones' monsters and +1 level, 40 with the mini-boss; overtime after the checkpoint ×(1 + 0.15 per minute) up to ×2, +2 then +3 levels, a champion pack every 45 s), spawns every half second (missing monsters at the segment's rate, doubled below half the target, 8 at most) on a ring 14 to 18 units around the player, 40 % ahead of a moving player, pulled back inside the arena, never closer than 10 units, on reachable cells only; species of Levels.txt (weights by difficulty) and their sisters (`MonsterDef.alts`, as the zones do); champions at 2:30, encirclement at 3:00 (25 to 40 at 9 to 11 units), unique (or the zone's midway super-unique) at 4:30, champions at 5:45 and 6:40, the zone's super-unique with its group at 8:00 (its arrival kills the ordinary monsters within 8 units, with their loot), whose death is the checkpoint (milestone `survivals:cp:<difficulty>:<round>`) and ends the run (`runBoss`: portal to town); monsters left beyond 30 units come back ahead of the player; bodies leave after 3 s. Monster levels: Levels.txt plus 0 to 3 (the zone level raised for the time of the spawn), life softened ×0.7 for 45 s; no loot or experience multiplier (D2's treasure classes and level gap). Waypoint section (rounds cleared / open / locked, "Resume" = the first open round not cleared, the mode's settings) and round HUD (time, live monsters / target, next event). Measured headless (invulnerable Sorceress, no fight): Corpsefire at 8:00, 556 kills in a round at level 16. Tests: formulas, arenas (built and cut from a test map), a whole round, cap, determinism, registry.
- **Lot 276 (S3, cards, death, end of round, controls)**: level-up (rule `skills.offer`): three cards among the skills `Game.canLearn` accepts (class, required level, Skills.txt prerequisites, cap 20), weights 5 (already invested) / 2.5 (synergy of a skill owned) / 2 (new active) / 1.2 (new passive), one invested card when there is one, seeded by character, level, reroll and points spent; screen locked 0.8 s, "Reroll" free once a round then 500 × 1.5ⁿ × (difficulty + 1) gold, "Keep the point"; the tree is read-only during a round (`skills.manualLearn`), free in town. Death: new base field `RunDef.abandonOnDeath` (inert in the base; `enterTown` forgets the run instead of leaving it behind a portal): the round is lost, the level, gear and checkpoints are kept, D2's experience penalty applies (0 % in Normal); deaths in a round ease it. End of round (`run.complete`): time, kills, level, the loot left in the arena (best first by the loot filter, one tap or "Take all"), overtime or back to town. Controls (rule `game.autopilot`): semi-automatic (joystick moves; standing still, buffs, auras and summons kept up by the brain's `maintain`, then the main attack at the automatically aimed target when in reach; a potion below 35 % of life; HUD switch "Auto attack"), automatic (bot brain plus a survival escape), manual (nothing added); stat points by profile (bot spending, all in vitality, or by hand). Calibration measured with the bots (headless, Sorceress / Paladin / Barbarian from level 1): at the recommended level, Act I's monsters of the engine kill the base's bots in one to three minutes past 10 to 15 live monsters, and nearly every death from the Cold Plains on came from ranged monsters (casters, archers) shooting from every side of the open arena: pressure by level (25 % of the target table at the recommended level, + 5 points a level, all of it 15 levels above; ×0.8 per death in the round, 10 % at least: lot 277's values) and ranged monsters drawn again three times out of four.
- **Lot 277 (S4, vertical slice of Act I, portrait and landscape)**: bot tests of the mode (`mods/survivals/tests/bots`: a new Sorceress, Paladin and Barbarian in automatic control, settings of the application, clear Act I Normal within 12 h of play; 97 s for the three). Measured on 12 characters (4 names × 3 classes): 11 clear Act I, most in 3 h to 5 h 40 of play with 35 to 75 deaths, one Barbarian in 10 h 28 (219 deaths), one Paladin stuck in the Jail and Cathedral round at level 20 (no gold for potions; wraiths and skeleton archers). Found by the measures and fixed: a stale survival-escape timer from the previous round (ticks restart with each world) froze melee bots for minutes; the brain's melee approach could swing for hours at monsters keeping their distance (automatic melee fighter of the mode: strike in reach, walk around the obstacles, give up a target not reached in 3 s for 8 s, no Sacrifice below 60 % of life); a Paladin killed itself with Sacrifice. Waypoint: mod sections first, in both views (the Rogue Encampment opens on the campaign view, where they were missing). Browser test `tools/e2e/survivals.mjs` (full build): mode at launch, character in `d2+survivals`, no rotate prompt in portrait, fullscreen lock `any` (never `landscape`), camera 0.72, round from the waypoint, HUD inside the screen with buttons ≥ 44 px and cards (3, stacked in portrait, side by side in landscape, ≥ 64 px high, buttons ≥ 44 px) on the phone (390 × 844, 844 × 390) and Galaxy Z Fold sizes (344 × 882, 882 × 344, 884 × 1104, 1104 × 884), the device turned six times mid-round (same world, clock going, never paused), a card taken, Corpsefire killed (end-of-round screen, checkpoint), death (back in town, no portal), 0 console error; screenshots in local, unversioned notes. Cost with about 90 live monsters, measured headless on a 16-core machine (`mods/survivals/tools/bench.ts`, Node, one core): 0.35 ms a step on average (p95 0.49) not fighting, 0.44 to 0.71 ms (p95 1.1 to 1.3) with the bot fighting, 0.9 to 1.8 % of a core at 25 Hz; 120 monsters: 0.60 ms (1.5 %). Rendering measured only in Chromium headless with software rendering (relative, see lot 278); on a real phone: not measured (lot S5, the owner's Galaxy Z Fold). `ruleset-choice.mjs` accepts the game modes after the content rulesets. Measured on 12 characters (4 names × Sorceress, Paladin, Barbarian, automatic control, from level 1): 11 clear the Normal campaign in 10.0 to 35.2 h of play (90 to 459 deaths, level 39 to 43 at Baal; 95 to 309 s of computation each), one Barbarian stays in Travincal (635 deaths); the three of the nightly test (`Bot …`): Sorceress 19.4 h, Paladin 17.8 h, Barbarian 19.0 h. Hell blockers found by the bots and fixed in the director's assists of a round already lost: the elite packs' members (escorts of the mini-bosses and waves, champions, minions) get the leader's life mercy (×0.85 a death, 3 % at least; measured: two Council members at full life held an Amazon of level 53 for 25 minutes in the Durance, Lister's minions killed a Barbarian of level 63 154 times at Baal), bosses and elite packs lose 3 resistance points a death (never below 0; Mephisto's 75 % in Hell), an elite wave not killed in 3 minutes withdraws, the guards and the waves' survivors leave when the act's boss comes (Mephisto and Baal were losing 40 life a minute next to them), a round left after 5 minutes without dying nor clearing it counts as lost at its next start (milestone `survivals:open:<difficulty>:<round>`; a round resumed from the save is the same attempt: the harness's Amazon left the Durance 68 times at full strength), escorts immune to the build beyond the cap of immunes not kept; the gold is picked up anywhere in the arena (a Bone Necromancer of the bots opened Nightmare with 539 gold and no mana potion). Loot (Diablo II's tables, no multiplier, `data/loot.ts` from TreasureClassEx.txt and SuperUniques.txt): quest treasure classes of the five act bosses at their first kill in each difficulty (`Andarielq` … `Baalq`, `Durielq - Base`), super-uniques' own classes (`Act n Super A/B/C`, the `x` ones, `Council`, the Countess', Griswold's for the Smith, the Cow King's, `Act 5 Champ C` for Baal's subjects) instead of a unique's, the loot of a boss, named elite or unique told at its death (a champion's when rare or better), settings "Ground loot" (all / magic and better) and "Automatic pickup" (gold and potions / also runes, gems, magic and better within 4 units). Content: every super-unique of SuperUniques.txt met (new elites met halfway, one every 45 s, none after 6 deaths but the first: Coldcrow, Bonebreak, Griswold, the Cow King, Flamespike, Fire Eye, Leatherarm, Taintbreeder, the Tormentor, Winged Death, Riftwraith, Eyeback, Snapchip, Bonesaw, Axe Dweller and four in the Worldstone Keep); HUD: the next event's chip names the coming elite and pulses in its last 10 s, a bar (name, life) for each named elite and boss alive, the round's bosses first; Act IV's zones in the `levels` registry while the mode is active (`survivals:<zone>`, Levels.txt levels, `data/act4.ts`; the base's registry unchanged). UI: on a phone in landscape the HUD row goes under the zone's title when they meet (measured at each refresh). Measured on the 21 characters of lot 282 (3 names × 7 classes, automatic, from level 1, 40 / 60 / 100 h allowed): Normal 21 / 21 in 4.8 to 17.4 h, Nightmare 21 / 21 in 5.0 to 30.3 h, Hell 21 / 21 in 4.6 to 55.3 h (lot 282: 18 / 21, the longest 51.7 h); Hell alone from the lot 282 saves after Nightmare, with the assists only: 21 / 21 in 6.0 to 49.9 h. (2) Trees: the arenas are cut from the real maps, whose trees are structures of orientation 14 (`places`), mostly not blocking (Blood Moor window 97 % open with 277 trees: the clutter was visual). `thinTrees` (`sim/arena.ts`) leaves them undrawn inside the arena (new optional `ZoneDef.d2Map.hidden`, indices of `places` skipped by `D2MapView.build`; base absent) except about one in three in a 5-unit ring along the border (hash of the case and the round), frees the trunk of a case holding only trees (≤ 8 blocked sub-tiles; never a wall's case, never water, the map's own grid untouched: copies) and small trunk clusters left with nothing drawn around; trees beyond the invisible walls stay. (4) Orientation (owner: "the home screen forces the orientation, then it changes when Survivals starts"): the PWA manifest asked `orientation: landscape` (the installed app opened its menus in landscape, then the round's first touch locked `any`, i.e. the sensor); the manifest asks none now, `setOrientationPolicy('any')` unlocks instead of `lock('any')` (the device keeps its own orientation and the player's rotation lock), the base still locks landscape at a game's first touch (unchanged in game); the menus never lock (the first-touch handler exists only in game). The rotate prompt stays a base in-game CSS rule. (5) Found while testing: after a resize (portrait → landscape, fold → unfold) the light map kept its former frame and darkened only the former width (`lightRT.resize` in place); a new light map is made when the size changes (`GameRenderer.resize`). (6) Comparison with the owner's 8 screenshots of the other game (ergonomics only, nothing copied): done here: wider camera, open ground, level-up cards with the skill tree's first two figures (current → next, SDK `skillLines`) and a number (keys 1 to 3, R reroll, Esc keep). Not done (to decide): a "Banish" button (a skill out of the offers for the run: a director field saved with the round, the offer's weights), counters on reroll / skip (ours: one free reroll a round then gold, keeping the point unlimited), a strip of the build's skills on the cards (the D2 tree has no slot limit), "evolves into" lines (no evolutions in Diablo II's skills), a D2-style bottom bar with orbs and skill slots (our clean HUD kept: gauges top left, the owner asked for fewer buttons in lot 285), the character sheet, the inventory's tap-to-pick / hold-to-equip and the skill tree's three tabs in portrait (base panels, laid out for landscape: known limit), an automap button and a kills / gold counter in the HUD, the spawn ring (14 units) against the wider camera (monsters may appear at the top and bottom edges in portrait), a sharper picture at the "medium" quality (internal resolution 1: sprites drawn below their size). - **Lot 290 (S15, owner's four requests: gear without requirements, no skill button, the screen flipping at launch, updates)**: (1) Gear without restriction in Survivals (Run and Campaign, `d2+survivals` and `pd2+survivals`): generic rule `char.equipNeedsClass` (base `true`; `canEquip(…, reqs, cls)`, `canWear` reads both rules; without the class restriction an item for the mercenary alone still never goes on the hero); the mode sets `char.equipNeedsReqs` and `char.equipNeedsClass` to false and drops lot 289's "asleep" rule (`itemAwake` removed: worn gear always counts). Mod: the loot walked over goes on in an empty slot or in place of the worn item when clearly better (`autoSlotFor`, `gearScore` +5 % and 8 points at least; the class's items only, never another weapon kind or handedness); the bots' shopping (`SV_SHOP`) without the level cap. Balance (Act I in Normal only): lot 289's tuning now won 81 / 84 bot runs (96 %, later runs easier with the gear kept); Act I's own easing `RUN.mercyAct1` (damage ×0.4, bosses' and packs' life ×0.45, elites' damage ×0.6) brings it to 73 / 84 and 76 / 84 (87 %, 90 %), every class winning runs, ~27 min a won run. (2) No skill nor attack button in any control (`hudHidden`: `AUTO_HIDDEN` in a round and in town), the manual control removed (a saved one reads as semi-automatic; `tools/bench.ts manual` = semi without the automatic attack), no automatic-attack switch in the pause; the semi-automatic control casts the skill the brain finds the most profitable (`BotBrain.attackChoice`, new public wrapper of `bestSkill`) at a target in reach. Skills preview `ui/skillstrip.ts`: the active skills taken (6 at most) as icons at the bottom of the round, `pointer-events: none`, `aria-hidden`; after each automatic cast an icon darkens and fills back clockwise over its cooldown or 0.6 s, dimmed when the mana is short. (3) Orientation at launch, reproduced with Playwright (mobile emulation, `tools/e2e/orientation-launch.mjs`, the locks, fullscreen requests, resizes and the "rotate" prompt recorded): the fullscreen was asked at the game's first touch (the joystick as the run began: a resize, and an `unlock()` call in Survivals), and the "rotate" prompt flashed at each launch (index.html, before the stylesheet). Now: fullscreen at the menus' first touch (`App.start`), the screen locked only once a game is on screen (`setOrientationPolicy`, a policy never applied on the menus; `any` never unlocks what was not locked), `.rotate` hidden inline until the stylesheet decides; the base game still locks landscape, at its start. (4) Updates: a "Check for updates" button on both home screens (`ui/update-link.ts`: "À jour", "Mise à jour disponible", "Mise à jour…", "Vérification impossible"; a tap checks and installs a new version, the saves written first); at each launch the service worker is asked at once and a new version found is installed while no game runs (`canUpdateNow = !running`); in a run the update card waits for the run's end (the hub); offline no automatic install (the waiting worker does not activate during the launch offline, measured), the check is told impossible (Chromium's `update()` resolves offline), a version that does not take over within 20 s closes the progress window and stays offered, one automatic try a session (no reload loop). Controls: e2e `tools/e2e/update-check.mjs` (production build served by `python3 -m http.server`, versions published by rewriting `sw.js`): U1 up to date, U2 a new version installed at launch with one reload and no loop, U3 by the button, U4 none during a run then offered at the hub, U5 offline, storage keys unchanged, 0 page error; `tools/e2e/orientation-launch.mjs` (phone portrait and landscape from a pd2 session, the Fold folded from d2, unfolded from Survivals, the character resumed, the base d2 game) 34 ✓; `tools/e2e/survivals.mjs` E1-E2 (a level-1 hero and a Great Helm needing 63 strength: no red requirement, "Equip" open, its defense counted once worn), H1 (no attack nor skill button, the preview not a touch target, refreshed after an automatic cast), P3 (the pause without the switch), O1 (no lock nor unlock at a round's first touch): 185 ✓, 0 console error (Chromium headless, software rendering; screenshots in local, unversioned notes, `survivals/shots/290`); unit `mods/survivals/tests/unit/gear-free.test.ts` (both variants, other class's item, auto-equipment rules, pd2, without the mode), `cards-death.test.ts` (controls, preview), `content-loot.test.ts`, `runmode.test.ts`, `tests/unit/mods-gamemode-controls.test.ts` (orientation); fingerprints pd2 (9 sections) and d2 (8 sections) identical to d0a7f34 (worktree removed after); `test:private` both passes (2,322 / 43 skipped with pd2, 1,788 / 45 without mods); `npm run vanilla` 25 files identical, `mods:suppression` OK, `mods:residual` up to date, build, typecheck, ESLint, Prettier; unit suite 2,426 passed; save version 7 unchanged, storage keys unchanged. What is real: every action is a `Game` method of the base (`equip`, `unequip`, `stowCharm`, `unstowCharm`, `sell`, `sellCharm`, `stash`, `unstash`, `cubeAdd`, `cubeRemove`, `cubeFromTab`, `cubeMatches`, `transmuteGrid`, `transmute` with `cubeCheck`, `mercOffers`, `hireMerc`, `mercEquip`, `mercUnequip`, `resurrectMerc`); no new mechanic. Not real yet / not done: no balance measured with a hireling (the bots of `tools/tiers.ts` and `--run` hire none: a hireling makes lot 294's runs easier), corruption and maps not offered in Craft (the owner's endgame, later), the Campaign keeps lot 285's hub, no drag and drop in the hub (taps only), the trees read only (the Run's skills come by cards). Done: (1) balance, `RUN.mercyAct1.dmg` 0.7 → 1 (Diablo II's hits), `FLOW.classTaken` lightened (six classes around 1: Amazon 0.4, Assassin 0.95, Barbarian 1.2, Necromancer 0.9, Paladin 0.85, Sorceress 1.5, the Druid none), `FLOW.zoneDmg` the Dark Wood 0.8 and the lair 0.35, `FLOW.missileDmg` 0.55 → 0.45; (2) `FLOW.density` gentle first minute (5, 10 at 0:30, 16 at 1:00, 40 at 2:00, 60 at 3:00); (3) `FLOW.levels` (2 at 0:12 … 5 at 1:00, 8 at 2:20, 11 at 4:00, 18 at 12:00); (4) `ECON.zoneCap` 7 → 40; (5) the HUD's `kills` and `gold` chips in a run, the row hidden under the death notice, the run-end screen's killer line spaced, the longest run kept (`survivals:rec:<d>:<act>:surv`, a death included: "Nouvelle plus longue survie !", the records' list); (6) the bots banish a card off their plan in a run (`autoAnswer`: the bow Amazon never met Multiple Shot). Iterations (`tools/tiers.ts`, 6 then 8 runs a class and tier): before 267, 243, 401, 709, 721 s (tier 0 124-538 by class); 1: 152, 209, 380, 567, 718; 2: 156, 215, 337, 404, 708; 3: 209, 238, 258, 399, 562; then a pass on the late walls (ramp ×3 undone: Barbarian, Sorceress, Necromancer without a victory in 25 runs; the Dark Wood's archers): **206, 221, 264, 709, 713 s** (tier 0 170-218 but the Assassin's 558, two in eight won; tier 4 35 / 56 victories). Run after run (`tools/campaign.ts --run` Prog and Bis, 25 runs, `SV_SHOP=1`): 14 heroes of 14 beat Andariel, first victory at run 1 to 19, median 11.5 (lot 294: 13 / 14, median 8); the Druid and the Assassin still win from run 1-4 (kept for the next lot). Gold a run (`tools/gold.ts`, starter kit): 60-115 a run of 2:30-3:30, 280-460 a won one. Not done: the field a fifth darker than theirs (the base's lighting setting, hidden in the mode), the elites' affixes under their bar, their 6-minute act (ours 12, the owner's), their paid ranks (none, the owner's rule). Controls: unit `mods/survivals/tests/unit/lot296.test.ts` (4), `flow.test.ts`, `lot294.test.ts`, `shop.test.ts` updated, the unit suites pd2 2,477 passed and d2 1,797 passed; e2e `tools/e2e/survivals.mjs` 214 ✓, 0 console error (Chromium headless; R2 and R3 now answer every waiting card by a click in the page before the zone's end and the death: the quicker levels leave several, and Playwright's click waited 30 s on a locked card; earlier runs were flaky under a heavy machine load); fingerprints pd2 (9 sections) and d2 (8 sections) identical to 64ad542 (aucun écart; reference from the same tree with the lot stashed); `npm run vanilla` 25 files identical; `mods:suppression` OK; typecheck, ESLint, Prettier; save version 7 unchanged (one new milestone key). - **Lot 297 (the owner's report: the update check, "Searching…", sometimes very long, sometimes failing oddly)**: causes reproduced with a production build and a faulty server (`tools/e2e/update-check.mjs`): (1) `registration.update()` had no timeout: a slow or half-dead network (`navigator.onLine` still true) kept "Searching…" as long as the browser waited; (2) the whole new version's download (387 files, 6.3 MB, `cache.addAll` in the worker's install) was counted as "searching", cut at 60 s then told "Up to date" while still downloading; (3) a download that failed (one file) made the worker redundant and was told "Up to date"; (4) a tap before the worker's registration ended was told "impossible" at once, a failed registration stayed failed; (5) offline was known by `navigator.onLine` alone; (6) the install's files could come from an HTTP cache's older copy. Now (`src/platform/update-check.ts`, `registerServiceWorker`): the build writes `version.json` (outside the worker's cache and version, never answered by the worker, `no-store` in `vercel.json`); a check asks it with `cache: 'no-store'` and a cache-busting query, 8 s a request, one retry (offline told at once), compares it with the running worker's version (`VERSION` message; a worker older than this lot answers nothing: the browser's update then decides), and only a different version runs `registration.update()` (15 s at most); the download is followed by the worker's own `INSTALL_PROGRESS` messages (6 files at a time, each tried twice, hashed files already cached kept on a retry, the others `no-cache`), a download without progress for 30 s or a redundant worker fails; one check at a time (a second call joins the first); states `checking`, `downloading` (x %), `available`, `installing`, `restarting`, `uptodate`, `error` with a cause (`offline`, `unreachable`, `server`, `download`, `install`); the launch's automatic check is quiet (the button at rest, a failure not shown). UI (`src/ui/update-link.ts`, one control for the home screen and Settings › Avancé / Aide): the label by state ("Téléchargement 46 %", "Injoignable · Réessayer"…; the home link sized for its longest label), a carved trough under it with the gold fill of the install window (indeterminate glint while checking, static under reduced motion), `aria-busy`, a `progressbar` with `aria-valuenow`; the install window's bar a `progressbar` too. Controls: e2e `update-check.mjs` (its own server: delays, statuses, a cut connection, a cacheable `version.json`): L0 the launch check discreet, U1 up to date in 0.6 s, S1 a server held 25 s told unreachable in 16.9 s, S2 a cut request retried, S3 a 500 told, U2 installed at launch (one reload), U3 the download's percentage rising 0 → 98 (48 values) with `version.json` cacheable, D1 a failing file told then retried and installed, P1 Settings in a run (checking, up to date, unreachable), U4 none during a run then offered at the hub, U5 offline (the downloaded version installed, then "Hors ligne · Réessayer" in 37 ms), EN, storage keys unchanged: 21 ✓, 0 page error (screenshots in local, unversioned notes, `survivals/shots/297`); unit `tests/unit/update-check.test.ts` (17: timeouts, retry, causes, stall, the buttons' view, the DOM button, a tap joining the quiet check), `service-worker.test.ts` (+4: `version.json` never cached, `VERSION`, the install's progress, a file retried and a failed install resumed); fingerprints pd2 (9 sections) and d2 (8 sections) identical to cb74bc6 (aucun écart); `npm run vanilla` 25 files identical, `mods:suppression` OK, typecheck, ESLint, Prettier; unit suite 2,498 passed; save version 7 unchanged, storage keys unchanged. Changelog entry (FR, EN).
- **Lot 298 (the owner's tests of the Run, and the home screen's logo)**: (1) Every skill on its own cooldown: the base cast one skill at a time (a cast or strike held the next request) and lot 294's rotation fired only while the hero was free. Base hooks inert without a mode: rule `player.parallelCast` (`World.sideCast`: a skill asked during a cast goes off at once beside it, its cost, effect and cast event, the running animation going on; the weapon's attack, a melee strike and the same skill wait) and `skills.onCast` (every cast, main or beside). Mod (`FLOW.parallel`): the rotation fires beside a running cast every damaging skill on its own floor, the others every 4 s, the brain keeping the main cast; each floor set at its cast (`floorCast`, replaces `holdCast`). (2) The cadence: a spell's floor never under `FLOW.spellMin` 0.8 s and every other spell held `FLOW.castGap` 0.5 s after a spell (two spells a second at most); measured with the new `tools/rate.ts` (table in the mod's README: the level-3 Sorceress 3.02 → 1.98 casts a second, 9.8 → 5.7 missiles, Fire Bolt 1.37 → 0.8 a second; the level-12 Assassin walking casts 12 of 13 skills, before 9); the casters' `FLOW.classTaken` measured again with `tools/tiers.ts` (Assassin 0.8, Druid 0.9, Necromancer 1.05, Sorceress 1.1): medians of the classes, tiers 0-4, 203 / 227 / 264 / 566 / 722 s before, 204 / 227 / 344 / 565 / 722 s now, tier-4 victories 39 / 56 both. (3) Ways back: "Choix du personnage" and "Écran d'accueil" in the pause (confirmed, the run's rule told) and the hub's bottom bar; base host `UiHost.characterList` (the list opens directly after the reload, `launchMode.openListAfterReload`; the base's "Changer de personnage" too) and `UiHost.homeScreen`. (4) A run never resumed: `RunDef.resumeOnLoad` may be a function of the character (the mode's: `!isRunMode`, the Campaign's rounds still resumed); a run left open in the save (closed mid-run, or an older version's `run` state) is ended at the first step in town as left without its screen (`abandonInterrupted`: loot settled, records kept, level 1) and a new run of its act starts at once from its first zone at 0:00. (5) The logo's fire (owner, a Galaxy Z Fold: "no animation at all"): reproduced on art generated by an earlier version (a revision-1 `ui/logo-fire.png`, no `ui/logo.png`), which the client deliberately showed still (its halves misplaced, the letters jumping); the local import animated (e2e). Now the revision-1 sheet is rebuilt at load at the halves' DC6 offsets (`LEGACY_LOGO`, `legacyLogoRects`, `legacyLogo` in `src/ui/d2ui.ts`): 15 frames, letters fixed; under "reduce motion" the menu's sprites run at half speed. Controls: unit `mods/survivals/tests/unit/lot298.test.ts` (6: beside a cast, the floors and the gap over 30 s of three classes, `spellMin`, a run never resumed, the Campaign's still, the pause's and the hub's buttons), `director.test.ts`, `runmode.test.ts` updated, `tests/unit/export-ui-logo.test.ts` (+1: the rebuilt halves at revision 2's places); e2e `tools/e2e/survivals-298.mjs` (C1 23 skills, 95 % of the seconds with two or more; C2 1.8 spells a second, none too close; R1 level 13 → a new run at 0:01, level 1; N1-N3) 9 ✓, `tools/e2e/logo-fire.mjs` (PC, phone landscape and portrait, the Fold unfolded, reduced motion: 6 to 8 frames in 8 samples, the pixels changing, the box fixed; revision 1 rebuilt in the browser) 27 ✓, 0 page error (screenshots in local, unversioned notes, `survivals/shots/298`); fingerprints pd2 (9 sections) and d2 (8 sections) identical to cb74bc6 (aucun écart); `npm run vanilla` 25 files identical, `mods:suppression` OK, `mods:residual` up to date, typecheck, ESLint, Prettier; unit suite 2,505 passed; save version 7 unchanged. Changelog entries (FR, EN).
- **Lot 299 (the owner's returns on Survivals, sign-in turned off, faster checks)**: (1) Run-end screen ("I have to scroll to reach the buttons"): the title, a body (`.sv-end-body`: figures two or three a row, notes smaller, tighter under 520 px high, scrolling on its own if it still overflows) and the actions' bar always at the panel's foot (`.sv-end-acts`: shop, play again, hub on one row, ≥ 44 px); the panel never scrolls. (2) The modes apart: the base already listed, created and saved a character under its exact ruleset (lot 271's `charsForMode`): measured in a browser, each Survivals list holds only its own; the owner's list could not be reproduced. What the two modes still shared with their content was the shared stash: each game mode now has its own (`sharedKeyOf`: `d2m.shared.<content>+<mode>`, sync id `shared-<content>_<mode>`), first a copy of its content's stash when it does not exist (`SaveStore.seedModeStash`: nothing a Survivals character had put there is lost; the content's stash left as it was), then apart; the guild stays the content's. A save whose ruleset this version cannot read but whose modes it knows is read under its canonical id (`repairRuleset`, `SaveStore.list` / `load`: `survivals+pd2` → `pd2+survivals`, `survivals` alone → `d2+survivals`; a mod absent stays greyed, byte for byte). Save version 7 unchanged. (3) The character card: base UI slot `charselect.card` (`CharCardCtx`: the summary and the save's milestones, `CharSummary.milestones`), the mode's `ui/charcard.ts`: a Run character shows "Sorcière · Run", the farthest act and zone reached, the runs and victories, the best time or the longest run, in place of "Niveau N"; the Campaign keeps the level (EN + FR). (4) Sign-in turned off (owner's request): `SIGN_IN_OFFERED = false` in `src/platform/build-flags.ts` (`SYNC_ENABLED`): no "Se connecter" / "Synchroniser" on the home screen and the lists (both screens), no Synchronisation section in the settings, the sync engine never started (`SyncEngine.init`: no request to /api/sync); the code kept, the local saves, export and import unchanged; `true` brings it back outside the public build. (5) The Run's hub (owner's screenshots in landscape: "zoom out, smaller buttons, no scrollbars"): smaller type and buttons with their 44 px touch area kept by a transparent `::before`, the grids' cells sized to the panel's height too (container queries), the stats on two columns in a low landscape, the Craft tab one category at a time in a compact grid, thin scrollbars; measured before / after by the new `tools/e2e/hub6-fit.mjs` (six sizes, 800 × 360, PC: the equipment 152-192 px over its panel, the stats 225-271, the recipes 1,128-1,382 before; every panel whole after but the hireling's list in a low landscape; every target ≥ 44 px). (6) Faster checks (owner: "the tests take too long while the game is being built"): measured on a 16-core machine — unit suite 61 s wall (369 s of files; `campaign-reach` 54 s, the pd2 maps 44 + 30 + 15 s, `zone-monsters` 40 s, `zones` 31 s), typecheck 26 s, ESLint 21 s, Prettier 21 s, `vanilla` 5 s, `mods:suppression` 95 s, fingerprints pd2 602 s (bots 222, endgame 142, zones 109, skills 102) and d2 about 420 s, e2e `survivals.mjs` about 11 to 12 min (Acts II-V 109 s, R2 / R3 waits, Nightmare and Hell 56 s, the six sizes of each scenario). New: `npm run check:fast` (typecheck, ESLint and Prettier on the files changed since HEAD, `vitest related` on them: 35-95 s), `npm run check:full` (typecheck, ESLint, Prettier, the whole unit suite, `vanilla`, `mods:suppression`: about 4 min, CI and pre-deploy), `npm run e2e:survivals:quick` (`--quick=1`: every scenario in two sizes, Act IV and Hell only, no screenshot nor perf: 228 s instead of about 680 s to the same point), each e2e line timed (`[+n s]`), `npm run fingerprint -- --jobs 4` (sections in parallel, the longest first: pd2 220 s instead of 602 s, identical output); CONTRIBUTING.md "Testing". The full commands are unchanged and stay the CI's. Also found: R2 in `survivals.mjs` failed since lot 298 (a level-up card whose offer went stale under L4 kept the game paused): the test now keeps the point after three refused taps. Controls (targeted, at the owner's request): unit `mods/survivals/tests/unit/lot299.test.ts` (4) and `lot299-modes.test.ts` (2), `tests/unit/sign-in-off.test.ts` (3), `platform.test.ts` (+1, the mode's stash), `mods-ui.test.ts`, `pd2.test.ts`, `charselect.test.ts`, `sync-quota.test.ts` (the engine measured with the flag on) updated; the unit suite 2,515 passed once (before the hub's changes); e2e `tools/e2e/survivals-299.mjs` (E1 seven sizes, E2 a long run-end, M1-M3, K1 FR and EN, S1, 0 page error) 26 ✓ with the Diablo II art, `hub6-fit.mjs --strict=1` all ✓, `survivals.mjs --quick=1` 118 ✓; fingerprints pd2 (9 sections) and d2 (8 sections) identical to df0ec5d (aucun écart: the stash's key and the card are outside them); `npm run vanilla` 25 files identical, `mods:suppression` OK, typecheck, ESLint, Prettier. - **Lot 300 (the owner's returns on the Run, Act I: loot, gold, skills, inventory, countdown, next zone, bosses, levels)**: (1) Loot from the bosses (`FLOW_LOOT`, `flowDrops`): the fodder's items within a budget of the act's time (0.9 a minute, 2 waiting at most), its runes and gems within their own (0.12 a minute), an elite one item at most half of the time, a zone's mini-boss 2 items at least and Andariel 4, the first rare or better, a rune half of the time and always for Andariel; measured with the new `tools/drops.ts` (god mode, the whole act, median of 7 classes): d2 3.71 → 2.07 items a minute (fodder 2.06 → 0.80), pd2 17.4 → 2.79 (fodder runes an act 47 → 0: the owner's "many El and Eld runes"), every boss with loot. (2) Gold: the fodder's piles pooled (`goldPile` 10) and the piles within 2.5 units merged (`mergeGold`): d2 160 piles of 1.7 gold an act → 25 of 10.5 (at most 12 → 3 lying), pd2 593 → 54 (86 → 5); `tools/gold.ts` 117 → 94 gold a run. (3) "My fire spell is no longer cast when I have 3 spells": the rotation's cast log was kept by character with the world's ticks, a new zone's tick starting at 0 left a skill cast late in the previous zone out of the rotation for a whole zone (gaps of 95 to 190 s, 2 to 5 s now): the log is the world's (`castLog`); unit test with 1 to 5 skills over a zone change (fails at 3 with the old log). (4) The run's items to the bag at once while it has room (`keepInRun`), apart beyond; the pause tells the run's loot (`runLootNote`). (5) A 10-second countdown after a zone's mini-boss (`RUN.bossWait`, `zoneWaitLeft`, `skipZoneWait`, the HUD's `.sv-wait` button: a tap goes on at once; 4 s on the zone's time, `Director.endedByTime`). (6) "No assets for the next zone, an error": a map generated in the background then joined by the zone's urgent request and stopped for the urgent sheets ended false without a failure, listed missing by the preparation (base, `D2LocalGen.joinMap`: asked again once at the urgent priority); the named elites missing from a zone's species (`roundSpecies`: Blood Raven, Rakanishu's Fallen, Griswold, the Smith; now known when the zone is built); the next zone never prepared: base rule `game.nextZone` (null in the base), the app prefetching its map, monsters and effects (local mode), the mode naming the run's next zone (`nextRunZone`); `sheetsFor` core reads a mode's `monsterPool`. (7) Bosses as walls (`FLOW.boss`, `empower`, `bossPowers`): a mini-boss ×3 life and damage, Andariel ×2, the whole escort, 3 of its escort called every 12 s, a fury under 40 % (×1.3 damage, ×1.2 speed). (8) Levels to 30 by 11:30 (`FLOW.levels`: 5 at 0:45, 10 at 2:30, 12 at 3:30, 18 at 6:30, 24 at 9:00; before 18 at 12:00), a skill at 5 points at most in a run (`FLOW.skillRank`; the other game: 6 + 6 slots, evolutions capped; no slot cap here: the trees' prerequisites take points), the ramp 0.5 / 0.4 a minute to ×4 (before 0.4 / 0.25, ×2.6), `FLOW.classTaken` measured again. Curves (`tools/tiers.ts`, median seconds, victories, the highest level): before 203, 234, 238, 581, 708 s (1, 1, 0, 26, 32 / 56; level 12, 18, 16, 19, 20), now 189, 215, 362, 744, 753 s (0, 1, 3, 20, 40 / 42; level 12, 16, 25, 30, 30); run after run (`--run Prog 25`, `SV_SHOP=1`) first victory at run 17, 4, 6, 5, 8, 14, 18 (Amazon … Sorceress; before 20, 16, 5, 8, 11, 13, none), median 8. Controls: `npm run check:fast` (typecheck, ESLint, Prettier, 2,218 related unit tests), unit `lot300.test.ts` (11) and the updated `lot294`, `runloot`, `runmode`, `flow`; e2e `tools/e2e/survivals-300.mjs` (all ✓, 0 page error) and `npm run e2e:survivals:quick` (118 ✓, 0 console error); fingerprints pd2 (9 sections) and d2 (8 sections) identical to 9398ef9 (aucun écart: the base's changes are the local asset generation, the app and a rule inert without a mode); save version 7 unchanged. - **Lot 302 (Act I of the Run tightened after lot 301: the casters too strong, the act too easy)**: measured first (`tools/tiers.ts` 10 runs a class and tier, `tools/campaign.ts --run` five seeds a class, a trace by 30 s of a run's life, the monsters near, their life, the damage dealt and taken): the threat stopped growing at 5:00 (`ramp` capped ×4) while the hero grew to level 30 (damage about twentyfold from level 12): past the 3:00-4:00 crowd a hero won whatever his gear (the Druid 7 runs of 10 with the starter kit, the campaign's first victory at runs 1-4); more life or damage alone changed nothing late (the casters keep 10 units away and healed 3 % a second from their kills); Diablo II's zone steps were the walls (deaths at 3:50, 6:10, 9:40); the traps and the Druid's lasting fires on short floors (Fissure 44-48 % of his damage); the bot Paladin's 5 points in Sacrifice killed him. Levers, structural first (`FLOW`): `ramp` damage 0.6 → 1 a minute (×6 at most) and late curves (`ramp.lifeAt` ×45, `dmgAt` ×2.6, `speedAt` ×1.85 by Andariel; director `lerpAt`, the act's boss not ramped), the zone steps smoothed (`zoneDmg`, new `zoneLife`), a kill's heal 0.004 → 0.003, the field a little denser from 2:00, the lasting skills on floors of their own (`minCast`: sentries and wakes 2.0-2.6, Fissure 4.5, Molten Boulder 3.0, Volcano 2.6), the Paladin's Run plan (`RUN_PLAN`, `planOf`, `RUN_AVOID`: one point of Sacrifice then Zeal; Sacrifice's swing 1.2 s), then `classSpell` and `classTaken` measured again. No gear: 2:30-3:34 by class (lot 301's code, 10 runs: 2:08-12:19, 14 victories of 70 → 1); mean by tier 4:48, 7:39, 8:59, 12:15, 11:26 → 3:08, 4:44, 4:46, 10:15, 12:32 (tier 2 victories 34 → 0 of 70); first victory in 25 runs, median of five seeds by class: Amazon 3 → 9, Assassin 3 → 10, Barbarian 9 → 11, Druid 2 → 10, Necromancer 9 → 10, Paladin none → 23, Sorceress 3 → 14 (all: 4 → 11). Limits: tiers 1 and 2 about the same; the Paladin the slowest (one seed of five without a victory in 40 runs). Unit `lot302.test.ts`, `flow.test.ts` and `lot301.test.ts` updated, e2e survivals quick (118 checks) passing; README "Act I tightened". Save version 7 unchanged.

- **M10d (mercenaires vanilla) non fait** : Hireling.txt décrit des sous-types par difficulté d'embauche (Normal, Cauchemar, Enfer) et par paliers de niveau, avec des compétences que la simulation des mercenaires n'a pas (Vision intérieure, Inferno, Pointe glaciale, Armure gelée, Éclair chargé, Étourdissement, auras Prière, Gel sacré, Puissance) ; le modèle du jeu (types et variantes de PD2, tableaux PD2 par niveau sans difficulté, compétences et tables du wiki : `mercs.ts`, `mercs-pd2.gen.ts`, `merc-core.ts`) ne peut pas recevoir ces données sans un nouveau champ de sauvegarde (ligne d'embauche) et ces compétences (lot M9). Aucune donnée ni règle des mercenaires n'a été changée.

## Pièges connus (à garder en tête)

- Les chemins de sortie de l'importeur sont hors dépôt (`D2_PRIVATE_DIR`) ; ne jamais commiter de fichier généré.
- Les sprites pré-rendus (`public/sprites`) sont régénérés par `npm run sprites` ; leur volume pèse sur le dépôt.
- La suite `bots` est longue (simulations de plusieurs heures de jeu) ; la CI ne lance que le projet `unit`.
- Les tests qui lisent des cartes ou des archives locales se désactivent seuls si les fichiers sont absents.
- Une carte ou un test de combat de bout en bout peut dépendre du hasard et du temps ; relancer avant de conclure.

## Reste à faire

Publication : historique ramené à un commit unique dans le dépôt neuf `lucas-gp/d2mobile` (depuis le lot 323) ; `package.json` porte ses champs
`repository`, `bugs` et `homepage` ; les failles se signalent par le signalement privé de GitHub (`SECURITY.md`).

Voir `docs/internal/BACKLOG.md`. Décisions en attente côté propriétaire : nom de domaine public, hébergement éventuel (les
données dérivées des tables de jeu restent dans le dépôt, décision du 2026-10-16 : `docs/DERIVED-DATA.md`).
