(A2) The stall rolled again at every NPC contact with the same seed (`vendor:runCounter:level`): an item bought came back. `Game.visitVendor` (contacts, town entry) rolls it only on a change of act or difficulty (Diablo II's act hopping; a new game: a new `Game`); `refreshVendor` still rolls at once (game modes, tests), its seed now counts the session's stalls (the first stall's seed unchanged). (A3) Potions: the drops and the vendor wrote the tier (0-4) into `ilvl`, the belt read it as a level against 15/30/50/70: every potion Minor (25 %), hidden by `potionUpgrade` (on by default); four tables. One table (`src/sim/potion-tier.ts`, `POTION_LEVELS` 1/12/24/40/60, the drops' and the vendor's thresholds), the potion keeps its item level (drop: the zone's, purchase: the hero's, .d2s: `potionLevelOf`), the tier is read at pick-up; the setting `potionUpgrade` keeps its own table of the hero's level (1/10/20/35/50, unchanged: default settings kept, Survivals' balance measured with it); `validateItem` takes a potion's real item level. Diablo II picks the potion by the act's and difficulty's treasure class ("Potion 1-6"); left for the vendors' lot. (B1) Life and mana kept: `keepsVitals` / `carryVitals` (life, mana, the potion still flowing, poison) on stairs, exits, waypoints, the chambers, into and out of town, by the portal back after a death (the hero as he is in town); a Rush run, a round, an endgame map still start whole (both sides must be the Campaign or a town). Diablo II heals in town by its healer only: `HEALERS` (app.ts) Akara, Fara, Ormus, Jamella, Malah (Atma no longer, Fara now; the message named Malah), `Game.heal` also cures poison and heals the mercenary; a level gained heals; a death respawns whole. (B2) Zones kept for the session (`src/sim/zone-keep.ts`, `Game.kept`): a zone of the Campaign left by any way is kept as its `World` and taken back when entered again (`takeZone`: a joined neighbour already built, else the store; `reenter`: the hero made anew by `World.resetHero` keeping his identifier, mercenary spawned, at the way back to the zone he comes from, else at its waypoint, else at its start); the seam manager hands its chain's worlds to the store (`adopt`, `evict`) and takes them back (`keptSeamWorld`, not populated again); each kept world numbers its entities apart (`World.idBase`, `freeIdBase`, 0 when nothing is kept); a Campaign zone without a seed is built from `hashSeed(name:zone)` (the waypoint drew a random plan); at most 12 worlds and about 96 MB estimated (measured: 12 Act I zones ≈ 15.5 MB of heap and buffers, estimate 43 MB), the oldest first, half given up when Chromium's `performance.memory` passes 75 %; a new difficulty forgets them; not saved (save v7 unchanged): a reload builds a zone again from its seed, as leaving a Diablo II game. (C) Death in the Campaign: `deathGoldLoss` (progression.ts), Diablo II's single-player rule checked (Battle.net's guide quoted on the D2R forum: the level in percent, at most 20 %, 500 gold per level exempt, the stash untouched, the same in every difficulty; applied to the gold beyond the exemption); no corpse, nothing else lost (the rest of the gold, which D2 drops on the corpse, stays carried), the portal back kept; the message "your corpse and loot await you" (false) replaced; the death screen named Harrogath in every act: `deathLine` (hud.ts) names the act's town (no portal line where a death ends the run). Texts: the waypoint menu no longer says the zones are regenerated at each visit. Fingerprints (reference HEAD 6a5d6d0, same machine, D2 maps; d2 without `endgame`, pd2-only): registries, chars, mercs, saves, zones identical in both; loot pd2 768 / d2 1462 (the potions' item level), skills 294 / 207, endgame 189 (pd2), bots 269 / 261. Attributed by a reference patched with the potion change alone: loot entirely the potions; the rest of skills the stall no longer rolled at each town entry (the global item counter shifts the items' identifiers, the skills' arena enters town once); bots: the reports of every Rush run identical to the potion-only reference, only their state hashes moved (identifiers), the reports of the two Act IV zones of the bots' plan (`a4_plains`, `a4_chaos`, Campaign zones) changed by their fixed plan per hero (and the state of the following runs of the same process by the item counter); endgame: 24 map runs' state hashes, their reports identical. Tests: unit `lot316-campaign-state` (15: the stall, the potions, life and mana, zones kept, the store's limits and identifiers, the gold rule, a death, the death line in every act; on the D2 maps: Duriel and Baal killed through their chambers, the Rush chambers, the Blood Moor and its joined neighbour kept), `potion-upgrade` updated; the whole unit suite with (2 622) and without mods (1 870); e2e `tools/e2e/campaign-state-316.mjs` (stall, potion, the Cold Plains down a cave and back, the camp and Akara, a death, Duriel and Baal) passes d2 and pd2 touch 915×412 and d2 mouse; `campaign-waypoints-315`, `campaign-town-exit`, `interactions-311` (one walk timeout, passed on the rerun), `campaign-seams-311` pass (d2 touch). Bots project: all pass but `mods/survivals/tests/bots/run.test.ts` (8, no victory in 6 runs), which fails the same at HEAD 6a5d6d0 (checked in a worktree): not this lot. Changelog (FR, EN), ARCHITECTURE.md ("Zones joined edge to edge… zones kept for the session").
- **Lot 317 (owner: "regenerate the imported maps to correct the waypoints", after lot 315's limits)**: the importer gives every zone of Diablo II with a waypoint its lvlprest waypoint room, floor and pad under the waypoint's DS1 object, and none to the zones without one (every lvlprest DS1 scanned for a waypoint object). Mazes: a room flagged `make` is made when no free room has its shape (a dead end hung on a free room; a four-way room opened on four sides; own generator `<id>:fit`, other mazes unchanged): Jail 1 `JailXWay`, Halls of the Dead 2 `TombXWaypoint`, Durance 2 `MephXWarp` (moved from Durance 1, where it never fitted), Worldstone Keep 2 `baalXway` (moved from Keep 1, which held it), the Arcane Sanctuary's centre `SanctNSEW4` (the arrival, anchor `prev`) and only the rooms' first drawings: their numbered variants are broken walkways that cut the maze into islands (2 % walkable, the centre out of reach; now 8 %, one piece). Outdoors: Lower and Upper Kurast `Burbs08x08_Waypoint` by the entry; the Blood Moor, Tamoe and the Rocky Waste a plain piece in place of their waypoint room (fixed: same draw; the Rocky Waste's an Act II DS1, the first piece gives the map its palette). Act IV: the City's `WarpLava` laid `over` the SE mesa room's far corner (new `over` part flag of `mergeDs1`), anchors prev/next; the River's bridge `BridgeLava` (its waypoint on it) hung south of the third room (`LavaNSW`), the way on to the Chaos Sanctuary at its far end (anchor `chaos`, act4.ts), Hephasto kept at the forge. The Halls of Pain: SW room `tempSWWay`. 16 maps regenerated on disk (~/d2-private/out, old files kept in ~/d2-private/backup-lot317; Durance 1 and Claw Viper 1 byte-identical; the 6 unreferenced `tmp_*` maps moved out of the output folder); d2requires.gen.json unchanged (maps' archives measured: same). Devices: `MAP_REVISIONS` (tools/d2import/map-revs.ts) + `MapEntry.rev`: a map generated on the device by the earlier export is cleared at start and made again, that map alone (`staleMaps`; GENERATOR_VERSION unchanged). Fingerprints (reference: HEAD in a worktree on the old maps): pd2 and d2 identical but `zones` 117 = 13 zones × 9 (the 10 zones given their room, Blood Moor, Rocky Waste, Keep 1; Tamoe's map lost only the unused waypoint object and floor: zone identical); registries, chars, mercs, loot, saves, skills, bots (and pd2 endgame) identical. Save v7 unchanged. Tests: unit lot317-waypoint-rooms (6, incl. the maps), lot315 updated (the River's waypoint by its way on), full unit suite 2629 passed (the one failure, a former-name hit in a new comment, fixed); e2e campaign-waypoint-rooms-317.mjs (10 zones: sprite on its pad, free, touched → menu, active; screenshots) passes d2 and pd2 touch; campaign-waypoints-315 d2/pd2 touch and campaign-seams-311 d2 mouse, d2/pd2 touch pass. `tools/ci/suppression.mjs`'s vitest: `--pool=forks --maxWorkers=3` (vitest 5 has no `--poolOptions`).
- **Lot 318 (owner's screenshot of the home screen's four mode buttons: "two categories on the menu, Survivals or Normal, then Diablo II original or Project Diablo 2")**: the home screen asks in two steps. `modeKinds` (charselect-core.ts) groups `launchModes` by kind: Normal (the contents alone) first, then each game mode on at least one content (Survivals); the same full ruleset ids in the same order (`pd2`, `d2`, `pd2+survivals`, `d2+survivals`), so saves, `d2m.mode`, the reload to the picked mode and its list, `switchMode` and the character lists are untouched. Step 1: one button per kind (`data-act="modetype"`, `data-type` `normal` / the game mode's id), the remembered mode's kind marked `cur`; step 2: one button per game it is played on (`data-act="mode"`, `data-mode` the full id as before, its name the content's: "Project Diablo 2", "Diablo II (original)"; the remembered one marked), a Back button (`data-act="modeback"`), the title "<kind> — choose the game"; Escape goes back; the focus goes to the step's button (keyboard). A kind played on one game only is picked at once; a single kind (no game mode installed) keeps the one-step screen of one button per mode. A few words under each button (`.d2t.mdesc`, 37 px under the button on the 800 × 600 stage, buttons then 62 px apart; right under their button in the portrait dock, `menuTags`): Normal "Campagne et fin de jeu" (base), the others from a new optional `tagline` of the manifest and of `RulesetDef` (pd2 "Saison 13 : endgame, corruption, objets revus", survivals "Des runs d’un acte, vague après vague", d2 "Le jeu d’origine, sans modification"), translated (en.json, the manifests' `i18n`). The screen without the Diablo II art (charselect.ts) does the same with its own buttons (characters counted per kind). The `.d2b.main` class stays on the kinds. Order kept as before (the default content, Project Diablo 2, first). Tests: unit charselect (modeKinds, the classic screen's two steps, Back, Escape, a single-game kind) and charlist (the D2 screen's two steps, words under the buttons, Back, Escape, the remembered mode); e2e helper `tools/e2e/pick-mode.mjs` (`HOME`, `pickMode`, `pickFirstMode`, `listModes`, `openKind`) used by every script that picked a mode or clicked the first `.d2b.main`; new e2e `tools/e2e/home-two-steps-318.mjs` (Fold 344×882 and 884×1104, 412×915, 360×640, 915×412, 1104×884 touch; PC 1440×900 mouse and keyboard: both steps, words, Back, ≥ 44 px in portrait, nothing overlapping, Enter/Escape, the mode remembered and marked at the next launch) passes in English and French; home-portrait, ruleset-choice, charselect pass; survivals-298 fails only on N3 (the hub's bottom bar has had a third button, "Choisir l’acte", since lot 306: not this lot). Full unit suite passes (the former-name guard's hit in lot 317's journal line, reworded). UI only: no fingerprint concerned, save v7 unchanged.
- **Lot 319 (audit of the Campaign against Diablo II, lots D and F; d2 and pd2, Rush, endgame and Survivals untouched)**: D. no Rush leaking into the Campaign. The waypoint in a campaign town or zone (`campaignActive`) is the Campaign's only: no "Rush — bosses and runs" tab (no jump to Baal, Diablo, the Countess or the Cows), act tabs `Game.openActs(d)` (Act I, then each act whose `actLock` is open at the difficulty shown), no level asked any more (`campaignZones` lost its `recLevel` gate: Diablo II has none), opened on the act and difficulty played; Harrogath (the Rush's hub, `campaignActive` null) keeps both views. Transporters (`TRAVELERS`, data/quests.ts; `Game.travelOffer`/`travel`, `App.traveler`): Warriv (camp → Lut Gholein after Andariel; Lut Gholein → camp), Meshif (Lut Gholein → Kurast after Duriel; Kurast → Lut Gholein), Tyrael (Fortress → Harrogath after Diablo); before their gate they only talk ("Listen", their line); no longer the whole waypoint menu. Maps of that act not on the device: the waypoint opens on its tab. Mephisto killed in `a3_durance3`: Diablo II's red portal to the Pandemonium Fortress (`act:act4`) instead of a portal home. Not done (Diablo II has none): Hratli, Cain or Jamella as transporters. F. quest locks (`QUEST_LOCKS`, `questLock`/`tryQuestLock`, checked in `enterCampaignZone` and `enterChamber` from a zone or town of the Campaign only): Stony Field → Tristram (`cain`, or the Key to the Cairn Stones: the Tree of Inifuss, now a quest object on its DS1 spot in the Dark Wood, gives the Scroll of Inifuss, Akara deciphers it, the key is spent at the portal, `gate:cairn:<d>`; simplification: the five stones are not touched one by one), Lut Gholein → palace (`tainted`), Valley of Snakes → Canyon of the Magi (`summoner`), the true tomb's door (`staff`), Travincal → Durance 1 (`khalim`), Frigid Highlands → Nihlathak's temple (`anya`), Arreat Summit → Worldstone Keep 1 (`ancients`); hint on screen (short, one line on a phone in landscape); every lock is lifted by playing; a difficulty below the highest beaten stays open (as `actLock`). Saves: v7 unchanged; `migrateQuestLocks` (every `Game`): a save without the marker `locks:319` keeps open each locked passage it already went through (`zone:<to>` milestone; Duriel's door: Duriel dead), recorded at its highest difficulty, then marked; new characters are marked at once. Not changed: `actLock`'s old rule (a zone of the act visited in any difficulty opens it) — so an act walked in Normal stays listed in Nightmare (audit 3.12, later lot); the Rush tips in tips.ts (audit 3.14). Fingerprints (reference: HEAD 7b0d60f in a worktree, same machine, D2 maps; d2 without `endgame`, as before): pd2 and d2 identical but registries 37 (the two quest items, `QUEST_LOCKS`, `TRAVELERS`, `q_inifuss`; `QUEST_OBJECTS` order), zones 18 (the Dark Wood: its tree, 3 difficulties × 3 seeds, maps and free), loot 1 616 (pd2) / 2 357 (d2) and saves 720: proved to be the two new bases (loot's shared generator and item counter: identical with them removed) and the marker milestone (saves identical with both removed); chars, mercs, skills, bots (and pd2 endgame) identical. mods/pd2 `vanilla-patch.ref.json`: the bases' hash updated for the same two quest items (identical with them filtered out, checked). Tests: unit `lot319-campaign-locks` (14: each lock shut with its hint then open by its quest, per difficulty, other exits open, Rush unlocked, Tristram by tree/Akara/key, migration, transporters, open acts, DOM waypoint without Rush tab and no level, Harrogath's Rush; on the D2 maps Duriel's door, each locked exit object, Mephisto's red portal), lot316 (Duriel's door now needs the Staff), full unit suite 2 645 passed; `npm run vanilla` identical; e2e new `campaign-locks-319.mjs` (waypoint without Rush and level, Warriv/Meshif before and after their boss and back, the six locks plus Duriel's door with their hints on screen, Tree of Inifuss → Akara → key → Tristram, Mephisto's red portal → Fortress, Harrogath's Rush tab) passes d2 mouse and d2/pd2 touch; campaign-waypoints-315 d2/pd2 touch, campaign-state-316 d2 (with the Staff placed first), campaign-waypoint-rooms-317 d2 pass.
- **Lot 320 (audit of the Campaign against Diablo II, lots E and G, points 3.12 and 3.14; d2 and pd2, Rush, endgame and Survivals untouched; the owner's decision: the Stony Field reaches the Dark Wood by the Underground Passage, no seam)**: E. The zones' graph as Diablo II's (`A*Step` exits): Act I the Stony Field's cave into the Underground Passage, whose level 1 is a passage (`prev` up to the Stony Field, `ahead` up to the Dark Wood, `down` to level 2), no seam between the two fields (`A1_CHAINS`: camp → Stony Field, Dark Wood → Monastery's gate, and the Burial Grounds joined to the Cold Plains' south: walked into, no red portal); Act II the Valley of Snakes a dead end, the Canyon of the Magi reached by the Summoner's red portal (`QUEST_PORTALS`, `Game.questPortals`: absent before the quest at that difficulty, opened by his spawn with a message when he dies, there at a later visit; the lock `canyon` now from the Arcane Sanctuary) and left by its waypoint; Act III the Arachnid Lair and the Spider Cavern both in the Spider Forest (the jungle maze's second clearing anchored `cave2`), the Swampy Pit beside the Flayer Dungeon in the Flayer Jungle, none in the Great Marsh; Act V the way up under the mountain (Arreat Plateau → Crystalline Passage → Glacial Trail → up onto the Frozen Tundra → Ancients' Way → up onto the Arreat Summit; the Frozen River, the Drifter Cavern and the Icy Cellar dead ends below, each ice cave given a `down` room: `make`), the Plateau no longer joined to the Tundra, the Tundra given its second cave (`NorthEntrance_Snow`), Abaddon on the highlands' hell warp, Nihlathak's Temple by Anya's red portal beside her in Harrogath once she is freed (`QUEST_PORTALS`; its way back a red portal to the act's town; the lock `temple` from Harrogath, `campaignHere` now Harrogath too). Exits: `ahead` (the maze's exit room, its map's `next` anchor, when a third warp is in another room), `up` (« Monter »), `walk` (a passage on foot). Arrival: a Campaign zone built fresh from a way of another zone has the hero at its way back (`Game.wayBack`; the Dark Wood at the passage's mouth, the Tundra at the Glacial Trail's), as a kept zone did. Not done (left to later lots): the Upper Kurast entrance of the sewers (one up-warp in the sewer map), the monastery's gate → cloister → barracks (2.8: no facing openings), Harrogath's gate to the Bloody Foothills (2.17: its gate west, the foothills' town end west too), Act V's red pits' own maps and red portals (2.18). G. Joints (`seams.ts`): `planChains` (an act's chains in order, an opening joined once), `D2_SIDES` for Kurast (north through each gate), Act IV (Fortress E → Outer Steppes, S → Plains of Despair, S → City of the Damned; River of Flame N → Chaos Sanctuary) and the Burial Grounds, a map's `gate<side>` anchor making the opening over the importer's gate piece the one joined (within 2 units, else within its piece). Importer: `outdoor({ gates })` puts Kurast's gate pieces in the border (Lower N `SlumsGateN`, Bazaar S/N `BurbsGateS/N`, Upper S `MetroGateS` and N `MetroGateN` 17 × 17, the Causeway's bridge meeting it); `mesaZone(…, gaps)` leaves a border cell as the mesa's own 8 × 8 ground (Diablo II's opened cliffs `Border<n>o` were tried: a winding 2-4 unit gap, two of them facing across a seam leave under a unit, the hero stuck) and puts `FortMesa` under the Fortress's gate (its ramp meets the gate, 2 units); the River of Flame rebuilt with its bridge north (Lava rooms in the map's lower-left cells come out unwalkable from the importer's lava test, Diablo II's `WarpMesa` room too: the River's way back up stays a portal at its arrival, `prev`), Hephasto at his forge's preset; `a4_chaos`, the Campaign's Chaos Sanctuary cut to 96 case rows so its entry walk reaches the edge the bridge comes to (`buildChaos(…, map)`, `withD2Chaos(…, mapId)`; without it, the Rush's `chaos` and a passage at the bridge's end); Act IV's zones built by `joinedFarm` (`A4Step`, `buildA4Steps`, `RunDef.buildSteps`). Maps regenerated on disk (~/d2-private/out, old files in ~/d2-private/backup-lot320): a1_underpass1, a3_spider, a3_jungle, a3_lower, a3_bazaar, a3_upper, a4_steppes, a4_plains, a4_city, a4_river, a5_crystal1, a5_crystal2, a5_glacial1, a5_tundra (`MAP_REVISIONS` 2) and the new a4_chaos (index.json lists it). 3.12: `actLock` by difficulty only (an act walked in Normal, or a difficulty beaten, no longer opens it in Nightmare; no migration: the bug was the old rule). 3.14: the waypoint tip of a Campaign town speaks of the act's waypoints (`CAMPAIGN_WAYPOINT`, en.json), no « Run done » tip nor run banner in a Campaign zone (`Game.inCampaignZone`). Fingerprints (reference: HEAD 182342e in a worktree, same machine, its maps = the old ones; d2 without `endgame`): pd2 and d2 identical but registries 79 (the steps, chains, `D2_SIDES`, `QUEST_LOCKS`, `QUEST_PORTALS`, Act IV's runs' `buildSteps`), zones 576 = (35 zones on the D2 maps + 29 without them) × 3 difficulties × 3 seeds (the zones whose exits, maps or seams changed; the Blood Moor and the Black Marsh by their neighbours' thinned trees drawn beyond the seam), bots 234 (pd2) / 229 (d2): the reports differ only in the bots' two Act IV zones (`a4_plains`, `a4_chaos`), the Rush runs' reports all identical and only their state hashes moved (the process's identifier counters after those zones); chars, mercs, loot, saves (v7 compatible), skills (and pd2 endgame) identical. Tests: unit `lot320-topology` (11), lot311/312/314/317/319 and quests updated (Act IV and Kurast crossed both ways in lot312's leg test), full suite passes; e2e new `campaign-topology-320.mjs` (37 checks: Stony Field → passage → Dark Wood, Burial Grounds walked, Summoner's portal → Canyon, Spider Forest's caves, Kurast walked to Travincal, Act IV walked Fortress → City, stairs to the River, bridge walked into the Chaos Sanctuary, Act V's way up, Anya's portal and back, Nightmare's waypoint, the camp's tip) passes d2 mouse and pd2 touch; `campaign-seams-311.mjs` (legs list with the Burial Grounds and a restart in the Dark Wood; in touch runs the ground items' labels let the thumb through: the loot of the monsters it kills fell under the stick, a tap on a label = pick it up) passes d2 mouse, d2 touch, pd2 touch (one flaky « monster jumped » at a crossing in 1 of 2 pd2 runs, as lot 314 noted); `campaign-waypoints-315.mjs` d2/pd2 touch, `campaign-locks-319.mjs` (the Canyon and the temple by their portals) d2 mouse and pd2 touch, `campaign-state-316.mjs` d2 touch pass. Changelog (FR, EN), ARCHITECTURE.md.
- **Lot 321 (audit of the Campaign against Diablo II, lots I, J, K, L; d2 and pd2; Rush, endgame and Survivals untouched; the owner's decisions kept: no portal by Tome, default settings unchanged, pd2's experience unchanged, a death costs gold only, the mobile comforts)**: I. Automap (5.6, 5.7, 5.8): the Survivals' fog of lot 307 (`Fog`, cells of 2 units revealed within 14) moved to the base (`src/sim/fog.ts`, exported by the SDK; `mods/survivals/sim/fog.ts` imports it, its tests unchanged) and used by the base's minimap in the Campaign (`automapMemory`: a Campaign zone or a town): `exploreAround` reveals the hero's world and the worlds joined to it (their coordinates), one `Fog` a `World` (`zoneFogOf`, WeakMap: a zone kept for the session keeps its map, a new game forgets it; not saved, save v7 unchanged); only explored walls, props and the objects of `AUTOMAP_KINDS` (waypoint, exit/portal, shrine, NPC, stash) are drawn, no monster, no chest, the mercenary in green; Rush runs, endgame maps (their own fog) and rounds unchanged. The big map is an overlay over the field (no panel, no border, `pointer-events: none`; the corner map or Tab closes it). J. Vendors (3.3, 3.13): `src/data/vendors.ts` (derived data, LICENSE-DATA.md and DERIVED-DATA.md) from the vendor columns of Weapons/Armor/Misc.txt 1.13c read from the player's MPQs and gathered by type by hand (types sold, class items of the hero's class for casters and smiths — a simplification: the tables have no column for them —, potion tier hp1-hp4 and Nightmare/Hell upgrades, keys, Tome, throwing potions (Lysander, Alkor, Malah: absent from the columns, our choice), gambling, repair); `Game.openVendor(npc)` gives each NPC his own stall (normal or magic, no rare, some socketed whites), kept until a change of act or difficulty like lot 316's; a gambler (Gheed, Elzix, Alkor, Jamella, Anya) rolls a new gamble stock at each visit; repair only at the smiths (Charsi, Fara, Hratli, Halbu, Larzuk); an NPC's potions at Diablo II's tier and price (`POTION_PRICES`). The generic stall (`refreshVendor`: bots, game modes, tests) is unchanged (seed, contents). Atma no longer trades; Asheara trades beside hiring, Anya gambles and keeps her maps (`NpcDialogOpts.extra`). Healers (3.4) verified: lot 316's `HEALERS` (Akara, Fara, Ormus, Jamella, Malah). Gambling (3.5): base `shop.gambleRoll` with Diablo II's odds (`GAMBLE_ODDS`: 0.05 % unique, 0.1 % set, 10 % rare; was 70/24/3/3). Mercenaries (3.6, 3.7): `Game.mercActsHere()` filters the hire list of the merc panel only (`mercOffers` unchanged for bots and modes): a town's act (Kashya, Greiz, Asheara, the Fortress), Harrogath the Barbarians plus the acts whose town is out of reach (act not open or maps missing), so a Rush-only hero still hires every kind; the rescue quest gives a free Barbarian (`freeMerc: 'act5'`; characters who already did it are not given one retroactively). K. pd2's « every hit interrupts » (`combat.playerHitRecovery → true`, left to pd2 by lot M13 so as not to move its balance then) removed: pd2 uses the base's 1/12 threshold, as PD2 does. New rule `potion.rejuv` (base: Diablo II 35 % / 100 %, no cap; pd2: 35 % / 65 %, at most 2 000, the old values moved to the mod). L. A weapon rack gives one weapon, an armour stand one armour, of the zone's level (`pickEquipBase(…, kind)`; was two draws of a chest's TC). Docs: ARCHITECTURE.md (lot 321), the « Décisions volontaires » table of the mobile comforts kept (audit §0) and the owner's decisions in docs/internal/AUDIT-COMPLET.md, linked from both READMEs; MODS-RESIDUEL.md. Fingerprints (reference HEAD f010e95 in a worktree, same machine, D2 maps; d2 without `endgame`): pd2 registries 25 (the vendors' tables, the rescue quest), endgame 190 (140 map runs, 50 duels), skills 5 (Barbarian passives under hits), bots 233, chars/mercs/loot/saves/zones identical — the combat ones the hit-recovery rule; d2 registries 25 (same), bots 1 (`cold/pindleskin/2`), all else identical — attributed to the rejuvenation rule (identical with the old D2 values patched back). Tests: unit `lot321-npcs-automap` (10), combat-systems, vanilla-combat, fidelity-lot1 (rejuvenation by ruleset), mods-gamemode-shop (odds), ui-panels (Larzuk repairs without keys, Malah sells keys); full unit suite with mods (2 667) and without (1 915); e2e new `campaign-npcs-321.mjs` (Charsi/Gheed/Akara's stalls and services, Akara's heal, Kashya's Rogues, the Blood Moor's automap without monsters and growing, the big map overlay, Anya's gambling, Qual-Kehk's list) passes d2 mouse and pd2 touch; `campaign-state-316.mjs` (Akara's own stall now) d2 touch, `campaign-locks-319.mjs` d2 pass; `campaign-seams-311.mjs` d2: minimap dots beyond the seams still drawn, one known flaky « monster jumped » at a crossing (lots 314/320); `merc.mjs` was stale (no NPC dialog, English UI): fixed for both, its « pike given » check still fails on the old panel layout (not this lot). Not done (M/L or risky, for later): 4.2 a hit interrupting a cast (casters' balance in the Rush to measure), 4.4 no skill point at level 1 and 4.5 a hard point per level above the required one (bots' plans, Survivals' offers), 4.8 belt capacity (saves), 4.10 gold cap (saves above it), 4.11 gold and item TCs, 4.12 weapon swap, 4.9 MonStats, 4.13 pd2's attack and block factors (to check against PD2); 2.28/2.29 group sizes from MonStats MinGrp/MaxGrp (data not imported, densities), 2.30 Hratli's spot (no preset in Kurast's DS1) and walking NPCs, 2.31 day/night, 2.32 light radius, 2.33 the Cube's place (Rush, Survivals), 2.35 locked and sparkling chests; 3.6 the mercenary's own XP and Hireling.txt costs; 3.8 Anya's personalisation and the Rite of Passage's XP; 3.9 Cain in the camp after Tristram, Nihlathak (Act V's vendor before the betrayal), Flavie, Kaelan; 3.10 Lam Esen's book in a random temple; 3.13 stamina, antidote and thawing potions, scrolls (not in the game), vendors' « max buy » cap and repair multipliers (npc.txt), the gamble price formula, recharges (no charged items); 5.14 quest log, 5.15 PC keys; the automap saved across reloads (Diablo II does not either).
- **Lot 322 (the owner: « keep a Rush tab when the waypoint opens, so the player may switch to the Rush at any time »; d2 and pd2; Rush, endgame and Survivals untouched)**: lot 319's point D partly undone: the waypoint of a Campaign town or zone (`campaignActive`) has its « Rush — bosses and runs » tab back beside « Campaign » (`waypointImpl`: the mode bar whenever the Campaign can be shown or the hero is in it; the view no longer forced to the Campaign at each render), opened on the Campaign as lot 319 made it (`Panels.show`: the act and difficulty played; reopened, on the Campaign again). Kept from lot 319: act tabs `openActs`, no level asked, transporters, quest locks. A Rush run started there (verified, no sim change): `startRun` keeps the Campaign zone left for the session, the run is apart (`campaignActive` null, no `passageLock`, no `zone:`/`wp:` milestone, its portal home), home is Harrogath (Rush's hub, both tabs as before), whose Campaign tab leads back to the zone as it was left, nothing lost, the lock still shut. As before lot 319 (and from Harrogath), the progression is the character's: a Rush boss counts for the Campaign quest of the same name (`goal.runs`, e.g. the Rush's Andariel opens Act II) — unchanged, not a lock bypass of this lot. The camp's waypoint tip (lot 320, no Rush words) unchanged. Fingerprints: identical by construction (only `src/ui/panels-world.ts`/`panels.ts` changed, outside every section's import graph; not re-run). Tests: unit new `lot322-campaign-rush-tab` (2: the tab in a town and two zones, opened on the Campaign, Rush ↔ Campaign; Countess from the Stony Field, kept zone, no milestone, Harrogath, back to the same Stony Field, Tristram still locked), lot319 (the Rush tab there, Campaign selected); full unit suite 2 669 passed; e2e new `campaign-rush-tab-322.mjs` (camp and Stony Field waypoints with the Rush tab on screen, tap Rush → Countess → portal → Harrogath both tabs → Campaign → Stony Field the same world, no progression lost, lock kept; `--portrait`: 412 × 915 in game shows the « rotate your device » prompt (d2/pd2 are landscape only), then the checks held sideways) passes d2 mouse, d2/pd2 touch 915 × 412 and d2/pd2 `--portrait`; `campaign-locks-319.mjs` (Rush tab in the camp and Lut Gholein) d2/pd2 touch, `campaign-waypoints-315.mjs` d2/pd2 touch, `campaign-state-316.mjs` d2 touch pass. Changelog (FR, EN), ARCHITECTURE.md.
- **Lot 323 (owner, after the tour of the public build: « fix the 3 defects » and « disable playing without the game files »)**: (1) No play without the Diablo II graphics outside development: `D2_SKIP_ALLOWED` (`src/render/assets.ts`) is true only for a development build (no `D2_PUBLIC`, no encrypted folder) or a test build `D2_ALLOW_SKIP=1` (`__D2_ALLOW_SKIP__`, vite.config.ts; e2e scripts without MPQs, never deployed); `showD2Choice` shows the « replacement graphics » link only with `allowSkip` (first launch, files to provide again in `App.promptFiles`, the encrypted folder's password screen); `unlockD2` asks the password again until it is right or the player picks the import (before: three tries, then the game without the graphics); an old remembered refusal (`d2m.d2pass = '-'`) and the tab's skip (`d2m.d2skip`) are ignored. The e2e scripts on a public build that pass the screen by the link (`no-mods`, `public-build`, `survivals-298/300/301`) document `D2_ALLOW_SKIP=1`; `d2-onboard` checks there is no link and that the screen comes back. README (« Your own Diablo II files (required) »), DISCLAIMER, ARCHITECTURE and tools/d2import/BROWSER.md updated. (2) Player names never translated: `verbatim(name)` (`src/i18n`) wraps a name in invisible Unicode isolates (U+2068/U+2069), which `tr` keeps aside (private-use tokens) while translating the rest; used for the character list and its delete confirmation, the hero sheet (both views), Settings' « Delete <name> » and the guild's name (« Tour » stayed « Tour », was « Tower »). `survivals-299.mjs` strips the isolates when it reads `.sel-name`. (3) `F{0} (clic droit : changer)` added to en.json (the F1-F8 tooltips). (4) `CreateOptions.campaign` (false without Diablo II files): Normal's tagline is « Fin de jeu » / « Endgame » instead of « Campagne et fin de jeu ». Unit tests: i18n (names, F-keys), charselect (tagline), charlist (confirmation). Pre-existing on Windows, unchanged: 8 tests of mods/survivals and mods/pd2 fail with « mod pd2 absent » (`provideNodeMods`), as at HEAD.
- **Lot 324 (before going public)**: (1) `new URL(…, import.meta.url).pathname` replaced by `fileURLToPath(…)` in the 11 files that built a path that way (`tools/mods/node.ts`, `tools/fingerprint/*`, `tools/ci/{suppression.mjs,pd2-residual.ts}`, `mods/pd2/tools/vanilla-diff.ts`, 3 tests): on Windows the path came out as `\C:\…` (« mod pd2 absent » in the mods tests, `npm run fingerprint` failing), and anywhere a folder with a space was percent-encoded. Two more tests that failed only on Windows: `doc-paths` (the mods' README paths had `\` separators, so the mod-relative paths were never tried) and `d2requires` (a symbolic link needs the developer mode: falls back to a hard link). (2) Dependencies: `npm audit fix` (lockfile only, versions within the declared ranges). (3) `docs/DERIVED-DATA.md` §0 now says what §1 already said: the Diablo II values were read from the game's tables by the project's generators, some official names from `string.tbl`, the PD2 values from the PD2 wiki. (4) `.gitleaks.toml`: the fake token of the unit tests is allowed; `.github/dependabot.yml`: npm minor and patch updates grouped in one weekly PR, the CI actions in one monthly PR. No behavior change: fingerprints identical.

(3) `mods/pd2/tests/unit/maps-pd2.test.ts`: the 13 zones' test gets 180 s (about 26 s alone, over 60 s under the full suite's load). No behavior change.

(2) Wording: docs, comments, e2e scripts, the in-game changelog and three UI texts (character screen footer, password card, incomplete MPQ message) reworded; `docs/EXPLOITATION-IP.md` keeps the client-address chain and the settings only.
